using SMARTtools.Engine;
using SMARTtools.Utilities;
using SMARTtools.Core.Utils;
using System;
using System.Windows.Forms;

namespace SMARTtools.Control
{
    /// <summary>
    /// CallbackRouter is responsible for routing UI-triggered actions
    /// (Ribbon buttons, menu items, etc.) into the SMARTtools engine.
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    ///   This class previously routed SMARTshare workflows and relied on
    ///   SMARTshare-specific EngineState flags (IsBusy, SetBusy).
    ///
    ///   SMARTshare has been fully removed from SMARTtools. Therefore:
    ///     • All SMARTshare workflow calls are removed.
    ///     • All busy-flag logic is removed.
    ///     • CallbackRouter now serves only SMARTtools workflows.
    ///
    ///   This file is intentionally simplified until the new SMARTtools
    ///   workflow model is finalized.
    /// </summary>
    internal class CallbackRouter
    {
        private readonly EngineShell _engineShell;

        public CallbackRouter(EngineShell engineShell)
        {
            _engineShell = engineShell ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(engineShell));
            Logging.Info("CallbackRouter initialized.");
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Ensures EngineShell is ready before routing.
        /// This now performs ONLY null-check validation.
        ///
        /// The old SMARTshare busy-flag model has been removed because:
        ///   • SMARTtools workflows are synchronous.
        ///   • SMARTtools does not run parallel COM operations.
        ///   • Busy flags were part of SMARTshare's workflow architecture.
        /// </summary>
        private bool IsEngineReady()
        {
            if (_engineShell == null)
            {
                Logging.Error("CallbackRouter: EngineShell reference is null.");
                MessageBox.Show("SMARTtools engine is not initialized.", "SMARTtools",
                                MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                return false;
            }

            // NOTE:
            // The old check:
            //     if (_engineShell.State.IsBusy) { ... }
            // has been intentionally removed.

            return true;
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SMARTshare (REMOVED)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        /// <summary>
        /// This method previously initialized the SMARTshare workflow.
        /// SMARTshare has been fully removed from SMARTtools, so this method
        /// now serves as a placeholder until the new SMARTtools workflow
        /// routing model is implemented.
        /// </summary>
        public void OnSMARTshareInitClicked()
        {
            Logging.Info("CallbackRouter: SMARTshare Init clicked (SMARTshare removed).");

            if (!IsEngineReady())
                return;

            // OLD CODE REMOVED:
            //   _engineShell.State.SetBusy(true);
            //   _engineShell.RunSMARTshareWorkflow();
            //   (busy flag reset)

            // PLACEHOLDER:
            MessageBox.Show(
                "SMARTshare has been removed from SMARTtools.\n" +
                "This button will be repurposed in a future update.",
                "SMARTtools",
                MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                MessageBoxIcon.Information);
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;

namespace SMARTtools.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// AnimationModel
    ///
    /// Authoritative representation of a PowerPoint animation inside SMARTtools.
    /// This class captures all animation-related metadata including type,
    /// duration, delay, trigger, and optional motion-path geometry.
    ///
    /// Used by:
    ///   - AnimationGeometryBuilder
    ///   - IssueDetector (timing, sequencing, accessibility)
    ///   - OGMBuilder (object-level animation mapping)
    ///   - ServerPayloadBuilder (payload reporting)
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// AnimationModel is NOT the DTO sent to the server. It is the internal
    /// representation used by the Engine. The DTO version is AnimationDTO.
    /// </summary>
    public class AnimationModel
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Identity
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Unique animation ID assigned by SMARTtools.
        /// PowerPoint does not provide stable animation IDs.
        /// </summary>
        public int AnimationId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Index of the slide this animation belongs to.
        /// </summary>
        public int SlideIndex { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// ID of the shape this animation is applied to.
        /// </summary>
        public int ShapeId { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Animation Type
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Logical animation type classification (Fade, FlyIn, Zoom, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public AnimationTypeENUM AnimationType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw PowerPoint animation name (e.g., "Fly In", "Fade").
        /// Useful for debugging and mapping.
        /// </summary>
        public string RawAnimationName { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Timing
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Duration of the animation in seconds.
        /// </summary>
        public double DurationSeconds { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Delay before the animation begins (seconds).
        /// </summary>
        public double DelaySeconds { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the animation is set to repeat.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsLooping { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of repeat cycles (if looping).
        /// </summary>
        public int LoopCount { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Trigger
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Trigger type:
        ///   OnClick, WithPrevious, AfterPrevious.
        /// </summary>
        public string TriggerType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the animation is triggered by another shape.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasTriggerShape { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// ID of the shape that triggers this animation (if applicable).
        /// </summary>
        public int TriggerShapeId { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Motion Path (optional)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// True if this animation includes a motion path.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasMotionPath { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Geometry descriptor for the motion path.
        /// Includes path points, EMU coordinates, rotation, etc.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData MotionPathGeometry { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Raw OOXML
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw OOXML for the animation node.
        /// Extremely useful for debugging and advanced diagnostics.
        /// </summary>
        public string RawOOXML { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Constructors
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        public AnimationModel() { }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents high-level metadata extracted from a PowerPoint chart.
    /// 
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - This model lives in the CORE layer (metadata + meaning).
    /// - The ENGINE layer does NOT classify charts and does NOT use ChartType.
    /// - The ENGINE flattens charts visually; it does NOT rebuild or interpret them.
    /// 
    /// Purpose:
    /// - Provide future SMARTtools modules (SMARTshare, Manager, Comply, Governance)
    ///   with semantic information about charts.
    /// - Support analytics, reporting, compliance checks, and future AI-assisted editing.
    /// 
    /// MVP1:
    /// - The Engine will NOT populate most of these fields yet.
    /// - These fields exist to make the platform future-proof.
    /// </summary>
    public class ChartMetadataModel
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Classification of the chart (Bar, Line, Pie, etc.).
        /// Defaults to Unknown until metadata extraction is implemented.
        /// </summary>
        public ChartType ChartType { get; set; } = ChartType.Unknown;

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of data series present in the chart.
        /// </summary>
        public int SeriesCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of categories (x-axis labels, segments, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public int CategoryCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Names of each data series.
        /// Useful for analytics, reporting, and future compliance modules.
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> SeriesNames { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Names of each category (axis labels, segments, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> CategoryNames { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the chart contains a legend.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasLegend { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the chart contains a title.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasTitle { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The text of the chart title, if present.
        /// </summary>
        public string TitleText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The position of the legend (Right, Bottom, Top, etc.).
        /// Stored as a string for flexibility across PowerPoint versions.
        /// </summary>
        public string LegendPosition { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Free-form notes for future analytics or AI-assisted interpretation.
        /// This allows SMARTtools to store additional metadata without changing the model.
        /// </summary>
        public string Notes { get; set; }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// MediaModel
    ///
    /// Authoritative representation of a media object inside SMARTtools.
    /// This includes images, videos, audio files, SVGs, icons, and embedded
    /// objects. MediaModel is intentionally explicit so that all geometry,
    /// metadata extraction, issue detection, and payload modules can rely on
    /// a stable, well-documented contract.
    ///
    /// Used by:
    ///   - MediaGeometryBuilder
    ///   - IssueDetector
    ///   - OGMBuilder
    ///   - ServerPayloadBuilder
    /// </summary>
    public class MediaModel
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Identity
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// PowerPoint's internal media ID.
        /// This is stable and used for mapping across the pipeline.
        /// </summary>
        public int MediaId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Logical media type classification (Image, Video, Audio, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public MediaTypeENUM MediaType { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // File Metadata
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// File extension (e.g., ".jpg", ".mp4").
        /// </summary>
        public string FileExtension { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// File size in bytes (if available).
        /// </summary>
        public long FileSizeBytes { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the media is embedded inside the PPTX file.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsEmbedded { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the media is linked to an external file.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsLinked { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Path or URL for linked media.
        /// Empty for embedded media.
        /// </summary>
        public string LinkedPathOrUrl { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if PowerPoint reports the media file as missing.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsMissing { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Resolution (Images & Video)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Pixel width of the media (if applicable).
        /// </summary>
        public int PixelWidth { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Pixel height of the media (if applicable).
        /// </summary>
        public int PixelHeight { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Duration (Video & Audio)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Duration in seconds (video/audio only).
        /// </summary>
        public double DurationSeconds { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the media contains an audio track (video only).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasAudioTrack { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Geometry
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Geometry footprint of the media object.
        /// Includes bounding box, EMU coordinates, rotation, etc.
        /// </summary>
        public MediaGeometryData Geometry { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Raw OOXML
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw OOXML for the media element.
        /// Extremely useful for debugging and metadata extraction.
        /// </summary>
        public string RawOOXML { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Constructors
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        public MediaModel() { }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO
{
    /// <summary>
    /// NormalizedParagraph represents a single cleaned, structured paragraph
    /// extracted from a PowerPoint text shape.
    ///
    /// This DTO is produced by TextNormalization and consumed by:
    /// - NormalizedText
    /// - OGMBuilder
    /// - IssueDetector
    /// - SMARTshare payloads
    ///
    /// It contains ONLY normalized text + bullet metadata.
    /// No geometry, no raw OOXML, no formatting objects.
    /// </summary>
    public class NormalizedParagraph
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The cleaned paragraph text.
        /// All whitespace, invisible characters, and stray formatting markers
        /// have been removed by TextNormalization.
        /// </summary>
        public string Text { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if this paragraph is a bullet.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsBullet { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Bullet level (0 = top-level bullet).
        /// </summary>
        public int BulletLevel { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Bullet type (dash, dot, number, etc.).
        /// This maps to your BulletType enum if you choose to add one later.
        /// </summary>
        public string BulletType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the bullet is auto-generated by PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsAutoBullet { get; set; }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ============================================================================
    /// NORMALIZED TEXT MODEL (Engine Layer)
    /// ============================================================================
    /// This module represents the *fully processed* text extracted from a single
    /// PowerPoint text-bearing shape. It is the bridge between:
    ///
    ///   - Raw extraction (TextData)
    ///   - TextNormalization (cleanup + bullet analysis)
    ///   - IssueDetector (formatting + content issues)
    ///   - OGMBuilder (geometry + text footprint integration)
    ///   - SMARTshare (server payloads)
    ///   - SMARTwriter / SMARTlayout (future AI + layout modules)
    ///
    /// WHY THIS MODULE EXISTS:
    /// -----------------------
    /// TextData is intentionally raw, unstable, and directly tied to PowerPoint’s
    /// object model. NormalizedText is the *stable*, *clean*, *engine-friendly*
    /// representation that downstream modules rely on.
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS MODULE DOES:
    /// ----------------------
    /// - Stores cleaned text (CleanText)
    /// - Stores cleaned paragraphs (Paragraphs)
    /// - Stores normalization metrics (Metrics)
    /// - Stores issues detected during normalization (Issues)
    /// - Stores identity (SlideIndex + ShapeId) for downstream mapping
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS MODULE DOES NOT DO:
    /// -----------------------------
    /// - It does NOT perform normalization (TextNormalization.cs does that)
    /// - It does NOT detect issues beyond empty text (IssueDetector handles rules)
    /// - It does NOT interpret geometry or layout
    /// - It does NOT interact with PowerPoint directly
    ///
    /// FUTURE EXPANSION:
    /// -----------------
    /// This DTO is intentionally designed to grow:
    ///   - Semantic tagging
    ///   - Readability scoring
    ///   - Compliance scoring
    ///   - AI rewriting
    ///   - Structural analysis
    ///
    /// The Engine layer will evolve, but this DTO remains the stable anchor.
    /// ============================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class NormalizedText
    {
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Identity (minimal + stable)
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Slide index this text belongs to (1-based).
        /// Required for mapping normalized text back to slide-level models.
        /// </summary>
        public int SlideIndex { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Shape ID this text belongs to.
        /// Required for mapping normalized text back to geometry + OGM.
        /// </summary>
        public int ShapeId { get; set; }


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Normalized Content
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Fully cleaned text after normalization:
        /// - invisible characters removed
        /// - whitespace normalized
        /// - line breaks standardized
        /// - trimmed
        ///
        /// This is the canonical text representation used by all downstream modules.
        /// </summary>
        public string CleanText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Cleaned + analyzed paragraphs extracted from the text shape.
        /// Includes bullet metadata, indentation level, and paragraph text.
        /// </summary>
        public List<NormalizedParagraph> Paragraphs { get; set; }
            = new List<NormalizedParagraph>();


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Metrics (Engine-only diagnostics)
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Internal normalization metrics describing cleanup operations.
        /// These metrics are NOT sent to the server — they are used by:
        ///   - IssueDetector
        ///   - SMARTmanager (governance)
        ///   - Developer diagnostics
        /// </summary>
        public TextNormalizationMetrics Metrics { get; set; }
            = new TextNormalizationMetrics();


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Issues (local to normalization)
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Issues detected during normalization (empty text, formatting problems).
        /// This list is intentionally local — IssueDetector will add more later.
        /// </summary>
        public List<Issue> Issues { get; set; }
            = new List<Issue>();
    }
}

namespace SMARTtools.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// SeverityPayloadModel
    ///
    /// Represents the severity breakdown of issues detected across the deck.
    /// This model is used exclusively by ServerPayloadBuilder to generate the
    /// final payload sent to the SMARTtools server.
    ///
    /// It is intentionally simple, stable, and predictable. The Engine never
    /// mutates this object directly — it is constructed at the end of the
    /// pipeline from IssueDetector results.
    ///
    /// Severity levels:
    ///   Info      - Non-critical observations
    ///   Warning   - Potential problems
    ///   Error     - Clear issues requiring attention
    ///   Critical  - Severe issues impacting usability or accessibility
    /// </summary>
    public class SeverityPayloadModel
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Severity Counts
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of informational issues.
        /// </summary>
        public int InfoCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of warning-level issues.
        /// </summary>
        public int WarningCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of error-level issues.
        /// </summary>
        public int ErrorCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of critical issues.
        /// </summary>
        public int CriticalCount { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Aggregate
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Total number of issues across all severity levels.
        /// </summary>
        public int TotalCount =>
            InfoCount + WarningCount + ErrorCount + CriticalCount;


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Constructors
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        public SeverityPayloadModel() { }

        public SeverityPayloadModel(int info, int warning, int error, int critical)
        {
            InfoCount = info;
            WarningCount = warning;
            ErrorCount = error;
            CriticalCount = critical;
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Interfaces;

namespace SMARTtools.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ShapeModel
    ///
    /// Authoritative, Engine-ready representation of a PowerPoint shape.
    /// This class is intentionally explicit and heavily documented so that
    /// every Engine module—geometry, text, media, placeholder semantics,
    /// animation, chart/table metadata, and issue detection—can rely on a
    /// stable, predictable contract.
    ///
    /// ShapeModel is the *normalized* version of ShapeData:
    /// - ShapeData = raw extraction (Core.Extraction)
    /// - ShapeModel = interpreted, structured, Engine-ready model
    ///
    /// Every Engine module touches ShapeModel in some way.
    /// </summary>
    public class ShapeModel : IHasGeometry, IHasText, IHasMedia, IHasIssues
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Identity
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Stable shape identifier (mapped from ShapeData.ShapeId).
        /// Used throughout the pipeline for correlation and OGM linking.
        /// </summary>
        public int Id { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Shape name as reported by PowerPoint.
        /// Often meaningful for charts, tables, SmartArt, or named placeholders.
        /// </summary>
        public string Name { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// High-level shape classification (TextBox, Picture, Chart, Table, etc.).
        /// Uses ShapeTypeENUM for stable identity mapping across the Engine.
        /// </summary>
        public ShapeTypeENUM ShapeType { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Geometry (via IHasGeometry)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Fully interpreted geometry model for the shape.
        /// Produced by GeometryBuilder and related modules.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData Geometry { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Text (via IHasText)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalized text model for the shape.
        /// Includes content, font families, and text metrics.
        /// </summary>
        public TextData Text { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Media (via IHasMedia)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Media metadata (images, videos, audio).
        /// Extracted and normalized by MediaBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public MediaData Media { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Placeholder metadata
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder is required by the slide layout.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsRequiredPlaceholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder contains no meaningful content.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsEmptyPlaceholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder contains meaningful content.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsActivePlaceholder { get; set; }


        /// <summary>
        /// True if the shape is a PowerPoint placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsPlaceholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Semantic placeholder classification (Title, Body, Picture, Chart, etc.).
        /// Uses PlaceholderTypeENUM for stable identity mapping.
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderTypeENUM PlaceholderType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Placeholder metadata (index, semantic role, OOXML attributes).
        /// Produced by PlaceholderMetadataBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderMetadataDTO PlaceholderMetadata { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Animation metadata
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the shape has any animation applied.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasAnimation { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Geometry and timing metadata for animations.
        /// Produced by AnimationGeometryBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public AnimationGeometryData Animation { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Chart metadata
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the shape is a chart.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsChart { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Chart metadata model (series, categories, layout).
        /// Produced by ChartMetadataBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public ChartMetadataModel ChartMetadata { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Table metadata
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the shape is a table.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsTable { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Table metadata model (rows, columns, cell geometry).
        /// Produced by TableMetadataBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public TableMetadataModel TableMetadata { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Issue tracking (via IHasIssues)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Issues detected for this shape.
        /// Populated by IssueDetector and related modules.
        /// </summary>
        public List<Issue> Issues { get; set; } = new List<Issue>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Read-only interface projection for IHasIssues.
        /// </summary>
        IReadOnlyList<Issue> IHasIssues.Issues => Issues;


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Slide reference
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Zero-based index of the slide this shape belongs to.
        /// Used for OGM linking and payload assembly.
        /// </summary>
        public int SlideIndex { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Constructors
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Default constructor.
        /// ShapeModel is typically created by the EngineShell pipeline.
        /// </summary>
        public ShapeModel() { }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// SlideBackground
    ///
    /// Authoritative representation of a slide's background inside SMARTtools.
    /// This class captures all background-related metadata including fill type,
    /// solid colors, gradients, picture backgrounds, theme colors, and raw OOXML.
    ///
    /// Used by:
    ///   - ContextSlides
    ///   - GeometryBuilder (for bounding box interactions)
    ///   - IssueDetector (contrast, readability, theme consistency)
    ///   - ServerPayloadBuilder (payload reporting)
    /// </summary>
    public class SlideBackground
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Background Type
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Logical background type:
        ///   Solid, Gradient, Picture, Theme, or Master-following.
        /// </summary>
        public string BackgroundType { get; set; }   // REQUIRED


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Master Relationship
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the slide background follows the Slide Master.
        /// </summary>
        public bool FollowsMaster { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Fill Type
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Fill type:
        ///   Solid, Gradient, Pattern, Picture, etc.
        /// </summary>
        public string FillType { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Solid Fill
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Solid color hex (e.g., "#FFFFFF").
        /// </summary>
        public string SolidColorHex { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Gradient Fill
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Gradient stop colors (hex values).
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> GradientStopColorsHex { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Gradient stop positions (0.0 to 1.0).
        /// </summary>
        public List<double> GradientStopPositions { get; set; } = new List<double>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Gradient angle in degrees.
        /// </summary>
        public double GradientAngle { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Picture Fill
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the background is a picture.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsPicture { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Source path or reference ID for the background image.
        /// </summary>
        public string PictureSource { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Image resolution width (pixels).
        /// </summary>
        public int PictureResolutionWidth { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Image resolution height (pixels).
        /// </summary>
        public int PictureResolutionHeight { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the background picture is tiled.
        /// </summary>
        public bool PictureIsTiled { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the background picture is stretched.
        /// </summary>
        public bool PictureIsStretched { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Background Style Index
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Background style index (PowerPoint theme reference).
        /// </summary>
        public int BackgroundStyleIndex { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Theme Colors
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Theme color name (e.g., "Background1", "Accent2").
        /// </summary>
        public string ThemeColorName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Theme color hex value.
        /// </summary>
        public string ThemeColorHex { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Raw OOXML
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw OOXML for the background node.
        /// Useful for debugging and advanced diagnostics.
        /// </summary>
        public string RawOOXML { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Constructors
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        public SlideBackground() { }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Interfaces;

namespace SMARTtools.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// SlideModel
    ///
    /// Authoritative representation of a PowerPoint slide inside SMARTtools.
    /// This class is intentionally explicit and verbose so that all geometry,
    /// text normalization, media extraction, issue detection, and payload
    /// modules can rely on a stable, well-documented contract.
    ///
    /// Every Engine module touches SlideModel in some way.
    /// </summary>
    public class SlideModel : IHasIssues
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Identity
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Zero-based index of the slide in the deck.
        /// </summary>
        public int Index { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Title text extracted from the slide (if any).
        /// </summary>
        public string Title { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Layout
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Logical slide layout classification.
        /// </summary>
        public SlideLayoutType LayoutType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw layout name as reported by PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public string LayoutName { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Background
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Background descriptor (color, fill type, image reference).
        /// </summary>
        public SlideBackground Background { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Shapes
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// All shapes on this slide.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ShapeModel> Shapes { get; set; } = new List<ShapeModel>();


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Slide-level metadata
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        public bool ContainsCharts { get; set; }
        public bool ContainsTables { get; set; }
        public bool ContainsMedia { get; set; }
        public bool ContainsAnimations { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Placeholder semantics
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// All placeholder shapes on this slide.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ShapeModel> PlaceholderShapes { get; set; } = new List<ShapeModel>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Required placeholders according to the slide layout.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ShapeModel> RequiredPlaceholders { get; set; } = new List<ShapeModel>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional placeholders.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ShapeModel> OptionalPlaceholders { get; set; } = new List<ShapeModel>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Placeholders that contain no meaningful content.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ShapeModel> EmptyPlaceholders { get; set; } = new List<ShapeModel>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Placeholders that contain meaningful content.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ShapeModel> ActivePlaceholders { get; set; } = new List<ShapeModel>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Semantic grouping by placeholder type.
        /// </summary>
        public Dictionary<PlaceholderTypeENUM, List<ShapeModel>> PlaceholdersByType { get; set; }
            = new Dictionary<PlaceholderTypeENUM, List<ShapeModel>>();

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the slide is missing required placeholders.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasMissingRequiredPlaceholders { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the slide contains more placeholders than the layout expects.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasExtraPlaceholders { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Issue tracking (via IHasIssues)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Issues detected for this slide.
        /// </summary>
        public List<Issue> Issues { get; set; } = new List<Issue>();

        IReadOnlyList<Issue> IHasIssues.Issues => Issues;


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Constructors
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        public SlideModel() { }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents high-level metadata extracted from a PowerPoint table.
    /// 
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Lives in CORE (metadata + meaning).
    /// - ENGINE does NOT classify tables and does NOT use TableType.
    /// - ENGINE flattens tables visually; it does NOT rebuild or interpret them.
    /// 
    /// Purpose:
    /// - Provide future SMARTtools modules with structural information about tables.
    /// - Support compliance checks, analytics, reporting, and future AI-assisted editing.
    /// 
    /// MVP1:
    /// - The Engine will NOT populate most of these fields yet.
    /// - These fields exist to make the platform future-proof.
    /// </summary>
    public class TableMetadataModel
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Classification of the table (Simple, Matrix, Financial, etc.).
        /// Defaults to Unknown until metadata extraction is implemented.
        /// </summary>
        public TableType TableType { get; set; } = TableType.Unknown;

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of rows in the table.
        /// </summary>
        public int RowCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of columns in the table.
        /// </summary>
        public int ColumnCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The header row text values, if the table contains a header.
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> HeaderRow { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        /// <summary>
        /// All cell values in the table, organized by row.
        /// Each inner list represents a row of text values.
        /// </summary>
        public List<List<string>> Cells { get; set; } = new List<List<string>>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the table contains merged cells.
        /// Useful for compliance and layout analysis.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasMergedCells { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the table contains a header row.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasHeader { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Free-form notes for future analytics or AI-assisted interpretation.
        /// Allows SMARTtools to store additional metadata without modifying the model.
        /// </summary>
        public string Notes { get; set; }
    }
}

namespace SMARTtools.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// TextNormalizationMetrics
    ///
    /// Internal metrics produced by TextNormalizationBatch.
    /// These values help SMARTtools understand how much cleanup
    /// was required, how aggressive normalization was, and whether
    /// the deck contains systemic text issues.
    ///
    /// Used by:
    ///   - TextNormalizationBatch (producer)
    ///   - IssueDetector (flags excessive cleanup)
    ///   - ServerPayloadBuilder (payload reporting)
    ///
    /// This is NOT the DTO sent to the server. It is the internal
    /// model used by the Engine. The DTO version is TextNormalizationMetricsDTO.
    /// </summary>
    public class TextNormalizationMetrics
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Character-Level Metrics
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Length of the raw text before normalization.
        /// </summary>
        public int RawLength { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Length of the cleaned text after normalization.
        /// </summary>
        public int CleanLength { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of leading/trailing whitespace characters removed.
        /// </summary>
        public int TrimmedCharacters { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of collapsed spaces (e.g., "   " ? " ").
        /// </summary>
        public int CollapsedSpaces { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of non-printable characters removed.
        /// </summary>
        public int RemovedNonPrintableCharacters { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Line-Level Metrics
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of normalized line breaks (e.g., CRLF ? LF).
        /// </summary>
        public int NormalizedLineBreaks { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of empty lines removed.
        /// </summary>
        public int RemovedEmptyLines { get; set; }


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Word-Level Metrics
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of duplicated words removed (e.g., "the the").
        /// </summary>
        public int RemovedDuplicateWords { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of punctuation fixes applied.
        /// </summary>
        public int FixedPunctuationIssues { get; set; }

        public int BulletCount { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Aggregate
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Total number of normalization operations performed.
        /// </summary>
        public int TotalOperations =>
            TrimmedCharacters +
            CollapsedSpaces +
            RemovedNonPrintableCharacters +
            NormalizedLineBreaks +
            RemovedEmptyLines +
            RemovedDuplicateWords +
            FixedPunctuationIssues;


        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // Constructors
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        public TextNormalizationMetrics() { }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Chart
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// CHART DATA POINT DTO
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Represents a single data point within a chart series.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - Extraction layer produces raw ChartDataPointData.
    ///     - DTO layer needs a stable semantic representation for IssueDetector,
    ///       ChartAnalyzer (future), and LayoutEngine (future).
    ///
    /// MVP PHILOSOPHY:
    ///     - Keep chart points simple.
    ///     - Do NOT include formatting (colors, markers, gradients) until SMARTlayout.
    ///     - Only include values, labels, and geometry.
    ///
    /// USED BY:
    ///     - ExtractionToDTOMapper
    ///     - ChartDTO (collection of series ? collection of points)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class ChartDataPointDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The index of the data point within the series.
        /// </summary>
        public int PointIndex { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The numeric value of the data point.
        /// </summary>
        public double Value { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The category label (e.g., "Q1", "Revenue", "North America").
        /// </summary>
        public string CategoryLabel { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The data label shown on the chart (if any).
        /// </summary>
        public string DataLabel { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw geometry footprint extracted directly from PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData RawGeometry { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalized bounding box (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public BoundingBoxSDTO BoundingBox { get; set; }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Chart
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// CHART DTO
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Represents a full chart object extracted from a PowerPoint slide.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - Extraction layer produces raw ChartData, ChartSeriesData, ChartDataPointData.
    ///     - DTO layer needs a stable semantic representation for IssueDetector,
    ///       ChartAnalyzer (future), and LayoutEngine (future).
    ///
    /// MVP PHILOSOPHY:
    ///     - Keep charts simple.
    ///     - Do NOT include formatting (colors, markers, gradients) until SMARTlayout.
    ///     - Only include type, series, geometry, and bounding box.
    ///
    /// USED BY:
    ///     - ExtractionToDTOMapper
    ///     - IssueDetector (chart overlap, off-slide)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class ChartDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The chart type (Column, Bar, Line, Pie, etc.)
        /// </summary>
        public ChartTypeENUM ChartType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The collection of series within the chart.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ChartSeriesDTO> Series { get; set; } = new List<ChartSeriesDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw geometry footprint extracted directly from PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData RawGeometry { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalized bounding box (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public BoundingBoxSDTO BoundingBox { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the chart is part of a group.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsInGroup { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The group ID if the chart belongs to a group.
        /// </summary>
        public int GroupId { get; set; }
    }

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// AudioEffectDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents visual effects applied to an audio icon on a slide,
//   including brightness, contrast, transparency, soft edges, shadow,
//   reflection, glow, and blur. Although audio is not a visual media
//   object, its icon behaves like any other shape in PowerPoint.
//
//   These effects influence readability, compliance, broadcast clarity,
//   and semantic interpretation.
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - Values follow PowerPoint's native ranges (0–1 or -1–+1 depending on effect).
//   - Effects for audio icons are separate from images/videos to maintain
//     pipeline clarity.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Effects
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO describing visual effects applied to an audio icon.
    /// </summary>
    public class AudioEffectDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Brightness adjustment (range typically -1.0 to +1.0).
        /// </summary>
        public double Brightness { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Contrast adjustment (range typically -1.0 to +1.0).
        /// </summary>
        public double Contrast { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Transparency level (0 = opaque, 1 = fully transparent).
        /// </summary>
        public double Transparency { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Soft edge radius (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double SoftEdgesRadius { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the audio icon has a shadow effect.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasShadow { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the audio icon has a reflection effect.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasReflection { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the audio icon has a glow effect.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasGlow { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Blur radius (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double BlurRadius { get; set; }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Metadata;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Group
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// GROUP DTO
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Represents a PowerPoint group object containing shapes, charts, SmartArt, or other groups.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - RawGroupData contains raw extraction data.
    ///     - GroupMetadataDTO contains semantic metadata (role, name, behavior).
    ///     - GeometryData contains the raw bounding box footprint.
    ///
    /// MVP PHILOSOPHY:
    ///     - Keep groups simple.
    ///     - Do NOT include formatting until SMARTlayout.
    ///     - Only include children, geometry, and metadata.
    ///
    /// USED BY:
    ///     - ExtractionToDTOMapper
    ///     - IssueDetector (group overlap, off-slide)
    ///     - LayoutEngine (future)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class GroupDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The collection of child objects inside the group.
        /// These may be shapes, charts, SmartArt, placeholders, or nested groups.
        /// </summary>
        public List<object> Children { get; set; } = new List<object>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw geometry footprint extracted directly from PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData RawGeometry { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Semantic metadata describing the group's role and behavior.
        /// </summary>
        public GroupMetadataDTO Metadata { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalized bounding box (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public BoundingBoxSDTO BoundingBox { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if this group is nested inside another group.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsNested { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The parent group ID if nested.
        /// </summary>
        public int ParentGroupId { get; set; }
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// AudioDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents an audio object extracted from a PowerPoint slide, including:
//   - Identity and basic metadata
//   - Playback settings (autoplay, loop, rewind, mute)
//   - Trim settings (start/end)
//   - Volume and playback speed
//   - Position and size of the audio icon on the slide
//   - Compliance, broadcast, semantic, and analytics hooks
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added in the future as needed.
//   - Consumed by SMARTcomply, SMARTshare, SMARTcast, SMARTmanage,
//     and the ExtractionToDTOMapper pipeline.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Media

{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO representing an audio object on a slide.
    /// </summary>
    public class AudioDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BASIC IDENTITY
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Unique identifier for the audio clip (internal or GUID).
        /// </summary>
        public string AudioId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Original file name or media reference (if available).
        /// </summary>
        public string SourceName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// File type (e.g., MP3, WAV, WMA).
        /// </summary>
        public string FileType { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // DIMENSIONS / POSITION (audio icon)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Width of the audio icon in points.
        /// </summary>
        public double Width { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Height of the audio icon in points.
        /// </summary>
        public double Height { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// X-position of the audio icon on the slide (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double PositionX { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Y-position of the audio icon on the slide (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double PositionY { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // PLAYBACK SETTINGS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the audio autoplays when the slide appears.
        /// </summary>
        public bool AutoPlay { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the audio loops continuously.
        /// </summary>
        public bool Loop { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the audio rewinds after playback ends.
        /// </summary>
        public bool RewindAfterPlay { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the audio is muted.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsMuted { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Playback volume (0–1 range).
        /// </summary>
        public double Volume { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Playback speed multiplier (e.g., 1.0 = normal speed).
        /// </summary>
        public double PlaybackSpeed { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // TRIM SETTINGS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Trim start time (in seconds).
        /// </summary>
        public double TrimStart { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Trim end time (in seconds).
        /// </summary>
        public double TrimEnd { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // COMPLIANCE HOOKS (SMARTcomply)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this audio clip has compliance issues
        /// (e.g., autoplay narration, muted content, accessibility).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasComplianceIssues { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance summary for audio issues.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceSummary { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BROADCAST / ROUTING (SMARTshare / SMARTcast)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this audio clip is relevant for broadcast scenarios.
        /// </summary>
        public bool RelevantForBroadcast { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional broadcast tag (e.g., "Narration", "BackgroundAudio").
        /// </summary>
        public string BroadcastTag { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional semantic category (e.g., "Narration", "SoundEffect").
        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional analytics score indicating clarity or importance.
        /// </summary>
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional free-form analytics notes.
        /// </summary>
        public string AnalyticsNotes { get; set; }
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// ImageDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents an image object extracted from a PowerPoint slide, including:
//   - Identity and basic metadata
//   - File type and raw dimensions
//   - Position and size on the slide
//   - Alt-text and accessibility metadata
//   - Compliance, broadcast, semantic, and analytics hooks
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added in the future as needed.
//   - Consumed by SMARTcomply, SMARTshare, SMARTcast, SMARTmanage,
//     and the ExtractionToDTOMapper pipeline.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Media
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO representing an image object on a slide.
    /// </summary>
    public class ImageDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BASIC IDENTITY
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Unique identifier for the image (internal or GUID).
        /// </summary>
        public string ImageId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Original file name or media reference (if available).
        /// </summary>
        public string SourceName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// File type (e.g., PNG, JPG, GIF, BMP).
        /// </summary>
        public string FileType { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // DIMENSIONS / POSITION
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Width of the image in points.
        /// </summary>
        public double Width { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Height of the image in points.
        /// </summary>
        public double Height { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// X-position of the image on the slide (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double PositionX { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Y-position of the image on the slide (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double PositionY { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // ACCESSIBILITY / ALT-TEXT
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Alt-text assigned to the image (if any).
        /// </summary>
        public string AltText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether alt-text is missing or insufficient.
        /// </summary>
        public bool AltTextMissing { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // COMPLIANCE HOOKS (SMARTcomply)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this image has compliance issues
        /// (e.g., missing alt-text, low contrast, inappropriate content).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasComplianceIssues { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance summary for image issues.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceSummary { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BROADCAST / ROUTING (SMARTshare / SMARTcast)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public bool RelevantForBroadcast { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional broadcast tag (e.g., "Logo", "PrimaryImage").

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// </summary>

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// VideoDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents a video object extracted from a PowerPoint slide, including:
//   - Identity and basic metadata
//   - Playback settings (autoplay, loop, rewind, mute)
//   - Trim settings (start/end)
//   - Volume and playback speed
//   - Position and size on the slide
//   - Compliance, broadcast, semantic, and analytics hooks
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added in the future as needed.
//   - Consumed by SMARTcomply, SMARTshare, SMARTcast, SMARTmanage,
//     and the ExtractionToDTOMapper pipeline.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Media
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO representing a video object on a slide.
    /// </summary>
    public class VideoDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BASIC IDENTITY
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Unique identifier for the video (internal or GUID).
        /// </summary>
        public string VideoId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Original file name or media reference (if available).
        /// </summary>
        public string SourceName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// File type (e.g., MP4, WMV, AVI).
        /// </summary>
        public string FileType { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // DIMENSIONS / POSITION
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Width of the video frame in points.
        /// </summary>
        public double Width { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Height of the video frame in points.
        /// </summary>
        public double Height { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// X-position of the video on the slide (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double PositionX { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Y-position of the video on the slide (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double PositionY { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // PLAYBACK SETTINGS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the video autoplays when the slide appears.
        /// </summary>
        public bool AutoPlay { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the video loops continuously.
        /// </summary>
        public bool Loop { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the video rewinds after playback ends.
        /// </summary>
        public bool RewindAfterPlay { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the video is muted.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsMuted { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Playback volume (0–1 range).
        /// </summary>
        public double Volume { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Playback speed multiplier (e.g., 1.0 = normal speed).
        /// </summary>
        public double PlaybackSpeed { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // TRIM SETTINGS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Trim start time (in seconds).
        /// </summary>
        public double TrimStart { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Trim end time (in seconds).
        /// </summary>
        public double TrimEnd { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // COMPLIANCE HOOKS (SMARTcomply)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this video has compliance issues
        /// (e.g., autoplay without narration, muted content, accessibility).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasComplianceIssues { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance summary for video issues.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceSummary { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BROADCAST / ROUTING (SMARTshare / SMARTcast)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this video is relevant for broadcast scenarios.
        /// </summary>
        public bool RelevantForBroadcast { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional broadcast tag (e.g., "IntroClip", "PrimaryVideo").
        /// </summary>
        public string BroadcastTag { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional semantic category (e.g., "Demo", "Interview", "B-roll").
        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional analytics score indicating clarity or importance.
        /// </summary>
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional free-form analytics notes.
        /// </summary>
        public string AnalyticsNotes { get; set; }
    }

using System;

using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData
{
    /// <summary>
    /// BroadcastDTO contains metadata used when a deck or slide is being
    /// broadcast, streamed, screen?shared, or presented in real?time.
    ///
    /// Consumed by:
    /// - SMARTcast (primary consumer)
    /// - SMARTshare (synchronization)
    /// - SMARTmanage (session logging)
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Pure data container (no logic).
    /// - Populated exclusively by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// </summary>
    public class BroadcastDTO
    {

        /// Indicates whether the slide or object is currently visible
        /// during a broadcast session.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsVisible { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The timestamp when the object or slide became visible.
        /// Used for playback logs and analytics.
        /// </summary>
        public DateTime VisibleSince { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The timestamp when the object or slide was hidden.
        /// If still visible, this may be DateTime.MinValue.
        /// </summary>
        public DateTime HiddenSince { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The broadcast channel or mode.
        /// Examples:
        /// - LiveStream
        /// - ScreenShare
        /// - Recording
        /// - PresenterView
        /// </summary>
        public BroadcastModeENUM Mode { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// A list of broadcast events associated with the slide or object.
        /// These are simple text descriptions (e.g., "Slide advanced", "Muted").
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> Events { get; set; } = new List<string>();
    }

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ComplianceDTO represents compliance metadata for a slide, object,
    /// or deck. This DTO is consumed by:
    /// - SMARTcomply (primary consumer)
    /// - SMARTmanage (workflow + compliance history)
    /// - SMARTshare (ensuring compliant content is distributed)
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Pure data container (no logic).
    /// - Populated exclusively by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// </summary>
    public class ComplianceDTO
    {

        /// The compliance level assigned to the object or slide.
        /// Examples:
        /// - None
        /// - Warning
        /// - RequiredFix
        /// - Critical
        /// </summary>
        public ComplianceLevelENUM Level { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the object or slide is currently compliant.
        /// SMARTcomply sets this based on rule evaluation.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsCompliant { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// A list of compliance issues detected.
        /// These are simple text descriptions; full Issue objects live elsewhere.
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> Issues { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        /// <summary>
        /// A list of recommended fixes or remediation steps.
        /// These are human-readable suggestions generated by SMARTcomply.
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> Recommendations { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        /// <summary>
        /// The timestamp when compliance was last evaluated.
        /// </summary>
        public DateTime LastEvaluated { get; set; }
    }
