using System.Collections.Generic;

using SMARTtools.Core.Pipeline;

// ROOT OBJECTS
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object;

// SLIDE-LEVEL
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Slide;

// TEXT (TextDTO lives under Shape)
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Shape;

// MEDIA
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Media;

// CHART
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Chart;

// TABLE
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Table;

// SMARTART
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.SmartArt;

// GOVERNANCE / COMPLIANCE / WORKFLOW
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Presentation;   // SensitivityDTO, WorkflowDTO, VersionDTO
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData;       // PolicyDTO

// GEOMETRY (if referenced)
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// DECK DTO (v3)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// DeckDTO represents the COMPLETE DTO footprint of a PowerPoint deck.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     The old engine scattered DTOs across EngineContext, payload builders,
    ///     and various modules. This made the architecture brittle and difficult
    ///     to evolve. DeckDTO FIXES THAT by providing a SINGLE, authoritative
    ///     container for ALL DTOs produced during extraction + mapping.
    ///
    /// ROLE IN PIPELINE:
    ///     Extraction ? Normalize ? Map ? Build ? Assemble ? Publish
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS CONTAINS:
    ///     ? ObjectDTO (root objects: shapes, images, charts, tables, SmartArt)
    ///     ? SlideDTO (slide-level metadata)
    ///     ? TextDTO (raw text extracted from shapes)
    ///     ? MediaDTO (image/audio/video metadata)
    ///     ? ChartDTO (chart metadata)
    ///     ? TableDTO (table metadata)
    ///     ? SmartArtDTO (SmartArt hierarchy)
    ///     ? SensitivityDTO (confidentiality labels)
    ///     ? PolicyDTO (enterprise policy metadata)
    ///     ? WorkflowDTO (approval + routing)
    ///     ? VersionDTO (versioning metadata)
    ///     ? ThemeDTO (theme metadata)
    ///     ? TransitionDTO (slide transitions)
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS DOES NOT CONTAIN:
    ///     ? Raw extraction (belongs in PowerPointContext)
    ///     ? Normalized pipeline state (belongs in EngineContext)
    ///     ? Geometry models (EngineContext)
    ///     ? Placeholder models (EngineContext)
    ///     ? Animation geometry (EngineContext)
    ///
    /// MVP PHILOSOPHY:
    ///     - Keep DTOs together.
    ///     - Keep pipeline state separate.
    ///     - Make EngineShellResult clean, predictable, and future-proof.
    ///
    /// FUTURE-PROOFING:
    ///     Fields may be added over time, but NEVER removed or renamed.
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class DeckDTO
    {
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // ROOT OBJECTS
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// All objects extracted from the deck.
        /// ObjectDTO is the ROOT DTO for EVERYTHING (shapes, media, charts, tables, SmartArt).
        /// </summary>
        public List<ObjectDTO> Objects { get; set; } = new List<ObjectDTO>();


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SLIDE-LEVEL DTOs
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// All slide-level metadata extracted from PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public List<SlideDTO> Slides { get; set; } = new List<SlideDTO>();


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // TEXT / MEDIA / CHART / TABLE / SMARTART
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// All text-bearing shapes (raw text only).
        /// </summary>
        public List<TextDTO> Text { get; set; } = new List<TextDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// All media objects (images, audio, video).
        /// </summary>
        public List<MediaDTO> Media { get; set; } = new List<MediaDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// All chart objects extracted from the deck.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ChartDTO> Charts { get; set; } = new List<ChartDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// All table objects extracted from the deck.
        /// </summary>
        public List<TableDTO> Tables { get; set; } = new List<TableDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// All SmartArt objects extracted from the deck.
        /// </summary>
        public List<SmartArtDTO> SmartArt { get; set; } = new List<SmartArtDTO>();


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // GOVERNANCE / COMPLIANCE / WORKFLOW
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// All sensitivity labels applied to slides or objects.
        /// </summary>
        public List<SensitivityDTO> Sensitivity { get; set; } = new List<SensitivityDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// All enterprise policies applied to slides or objects.
        /// </summary>
        public List<PolicyDTO> Policies { get; set; } = new List<PolicyDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Workflow metadata (approval, routing, state transitions).
        /// </summary>
        public List<WorkflowDTO> Workflows { get; set; } = new List<WorkflowDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Versioning metadata for slides, objects, or deck.
        /// </summary>
        public List<VersionDTO> Versions { get; set; } = new List<VersionDTO>();


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // THEME / TRANSITIONS
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Theme metadata applied to the deck or slides.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ThemeDTO> Themes { get; set; } = new List<ThemeDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Slide transition metadata.
        /// </summary>
        public List<TransitionDTO> Transitions { get; set; } = new List<TransitionDTO>();
    }
}

    /// <summary>
    /// DriftDTO represents platform drift metadata — differences in rendering,
    /// layout, spacing, or formatting caused by platform inconsistencies.
    ///
    /// Consumed by:
    /// - SMARTcomply (detecting drift violations)
    /// - SMARTmanage (tracking drift over time)
    /// - SMARTshare (ensuring cross-platform consistency)
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Pure data container (no logic).
    /// - Populated exclusively by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// </summary>
    public class DriftDTO
    {

        /// The platform drift structural data.
        /// Contains:
        /// - Horizontal drift
        /// - Vertical drift
        /// - Scaling drift
        /// - Rendering drift
        /// </summary>
        public PlatformDriftSDTO Drift { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether drift was detected.

        /// Examples:
        /// - None
        /// - Minor
        /// - Moderate
        /// - Severe
        /// </summary>
        public DriftSeverityENUM Severity { get; set; }

        /// <summary>

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData
{
    /// <summary>
    /// GroupDTO represents a PowerPoint group shape and its children.
    ///
    /// Consumed by:
    /// - GeometryBuilder (group geometry normalization)
    /// - OGMBuilder (object graph modeling)
    /// - IssueDetector (group-level compliance checks)
    /// - SMARTmanage (structure + hierarchy logging)
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Pure data container (no logic).
    /// - Populated by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// </summary>
    public class GroupDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The raw geometry footprint of the group.
        /// This is the GeometryData DTO for the group container itself.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData Raw { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The normalized bounding box of the group (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public BoundingBoxSDTO BoundingBox { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The list of child object IDs contained within the group.
        /// These IDs map to ObjectDTO instances in the ObjectGraphModel.
        /// </summary>
        public List<int> ChildObjectIds { get; set; } = new List<int>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the group is nested inside another group.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsNested { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The ID of the parent group, if nested.
        /// If not nested, this will be -1.
        /// </summary>
        public int ParentGroupId { get; set; } = -1;
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData
{
    /// <summary>
    /// HierarchyDTO represents the parent/child structural relationships
    /// between objects on a PowerPoint slide.
    ///
    /// This is the THIRD identity layer consumed by:
    /// - OGMBuilder (object graph modeling)
    /// - GeometryBuilder (group + nested transform resolution)
    /// - IssueDetector (hierarchy-based compliance rules)
    /// - SMARTmanage (structure logging + analytics)
    /// - SMARTcast (rendering order + flattening)
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Pure data container (no logic).
    /// - Populated exclusively by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// - Hierarchy is essential for resolving group transforms, nesting depth,
    ///   inherited geometry, and compliance rules.
    /// </summary>
    public class HierarchyDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The unique SMARTtools-assigned object ID.
        /// This ID matches IdentityDTO.ObjectId and ObjectDTO.ObjectId.
        /// </summary>
        public int ObjectId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The ID of the parent object.
        /// If the object has no parent (top-level object), this will be -1.
        /// </summary>
        public int ParentId { get; set; } = -1;

        /// <summary>
        /// The list of child object IDs contained within this object.
        /// Used by OGMBuilder to construct the object graph tree.
        /// </summary>
        public List<int> Children { get; set; } = new List<int>();

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the object is part of a group.
        /// Group membership affects geometry, compliance, and broadcast behavior.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsGrouped { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the object is nested inside multiple levels of grouping.
        /// Nested objects inherit transforms from all parent groups.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsNested { get; set; }
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// ImageCropDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents cropping metadata applied to an image in PowerPoint, including:
//   - Left, right, top, and bottom crop values
//   - Whether the image is cropped at all
//   - How much of the original image is visible
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - Cropping values are expressed in points (PowerPoint native units).

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO describing cropping applied to an image.
    /// </summary>
    public class ImageCropDTO
    {
        /// <summary>

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData
{
    /// <summary>
    /// LinkDTO represents hyperlink metadata attached to any object
    /// (text, shape, image, icon, SmartArt, etc.) inside a PowerPoint slide.
    ///
    /// Consumed by:
    /// - TextDTO (hyperlinked text runs)
    /// - ShapeDTO (hyperlinked shapes)
    /// - ImageDTO (hyperlinked images)
    /// - IssueDetector (link compliance rules)
    /// - SMARTshare / SMARTcast (broadcast link behavior)
    /// - SMARTmanage (link logging + governance)
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Pure data container (no logic).
    /// - Populated by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// </summary>
    public class LinkDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// True if the object contains a hyperlink.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasLink { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The raw hyperlink address (URL, mailto:, file path, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public string Address { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The display text associated with the link (if applicable).
        /// </summary>
        public string DisplayText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the link points to an external website.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsExternal { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the link points to a local file or network resource.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsFileLink { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the link is a mailto: email link.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsEmailLink { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the link points to another slide within the presentation.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsInternalSlideLink { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The slide number or ID if this is an internal slide link.
        /// </summary>
        public int InternalSlideTargetId { get; set; } = -1;
    }
}

using System;

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData
{
    /// <summary>
    /// PolicyDTO represents enterprise policy metadata applied to a slide,
    /// object, or deck. Used by SMARTcomply, SMARTmanage, and SMARTshare.
    /// Pure data container; stable contract.
    /// </summary>
    public class PolicyDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The policy category (Brand, Compliance, Sensitivity, Layout, Media).
        /// </summary>

        /// <summary>
        /// The specific policy identifier (e.g., "BR-001", "LAYOUT-05").

        /// </summary>

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the policy is mandatory or advisory.
        /// </summary>

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public bool IsCompliant { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// A list of violations detected for this policy.
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> Violations { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Recommended remediation steps for violations.
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> Recommendations { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Timestamp when this policy was last evaluated.
        /// </summary>
        public DateTime LastEvaluated { get; set; }
    }

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object

{
    /// <summary>
    /// BrandDTO represents brand?related metadata applied to a slide deck,
    /// slide, or object. This DTO is consumed by:
    /// - SMARTshare (brand consistency across shared decks)
    /// - SMARTcomply (brand compliance rules)
    /// - SMARTcast (ensuring brand fidelity during broadcast)
    /// - SMARTmanage (workflow, versioning, and brand audits)
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Pure data container (no logic).
    /// - Populated exclusively by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// </summary>
    public class BrandDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The primary brand color set applied to the object or slide.
        /// Uses ColorSetSDTO, a structural helper containing:
        /// - Primary
        /// - Secondary
        /// - Accent colors
        /// </summary>
        public ColorSetSDTO ColorSet { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The primary font set applied to the object or slide.
        /// Uses FontSetSDTO, containing:
        /// - Heading font
        /// - Body font
        /// - Optional fallback fonts
        /// </summary>
        public FontSetSDTO FontSet { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The brand theme name (e.g., "Corporate", "Modern", "Minimal").
        /// This is a human?readable identifier used by compliance modules.
        /// </summary>
        public string ThemeName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the object or slide adheres to brand guidelines.
        /// SMARTcomply sets this based on rule evaluation.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsCompliant { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// A list of brand compliance issues detected.
        /// These are simple text descriptions; full Issue objects live elsewhere.
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> Issues { get; set; } = new List<string>();
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object
{
    /// <summary>
    /// IdentityDTO represents the classification identity of any object
    /// extracted from a PowerPoint slide.
    ///
    /// This is the FIRST metadata layer consumed by:
    /// - ObjectDTO (root object metadata)
    /// - GeometryBuilder (object-type geometry rules)
    /// - IssueDetector (object-type compliance rules)
    /// - OGMBuilder (object graph modeling)
    /// - SMARTshare / SMARTcast (broadcast rules)
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Pure data container (no logic).
    /// - Populated exclusively by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// </summary>
    public class IdentityDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The unique SMARTtools-assigned object ID.
        /// This ID is stable within a single extraction session.
        /// </summary>
        public int ObjectId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The high-level PowerPoint object type (Shape, Group, Image, Video, Chart, etc.).
        /// This is the FIRST identity discriminator.
        /// </summary>
        public ObjectTypeENUM ObjectType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The PowerPoint shape subtype (Rectangle, Oval, Placeholder, etc.).
        /// This is the SECOND identity discriminator.
        /// Only applies when ObjectType == Shape.
        /// </summary>
        public ShapeTypeENUM ShapeType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the object is a PowerPoint placeholder (title, body, picture, chart, etc.).
        /// Placeholder classification is derived from ShapeTypeENUM.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsPlaceholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the object is part of a group.
        /// Group membership affects geometry, compliance, and broadcast behavior.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsGrouped { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the object is a legacy or unsupported PowerPoint object.
        /// Legacy objects may have limited geometry or compliance metadata.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsLegacy { get; set; }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Chart;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Group;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Placeholder;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Shape;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.SmartArt;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Metadata;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// OBJECT DTO
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// The universal wrapper for ANY object extracted from a PowerPoint slide.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - PowerPoint exposes many object types (shapes, charts, SmartArt, groups, placeholders).
    ///     - The extraction layer produces RawObjectData.
    ///     - The DTO layer needs a unified semantic wrapper for IssueDetector and LayoutEngine.
    ///
    /// MVP PHILOSOPHY:
    ///     - Keep object wrapper simple.
    ///     - Only include type, bounding box, and the specific DTO for the object.
    ///
    /// USED BY:
    ///     - ExtractionToDTOMapper
    ///     - SlideDTO (collection of ObjectDTO)
    ///     - IssueDetector (object-level analysis)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class ObjectDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The object type (Shape, Chart, SmartArt, Group, Placeholder, etc.)
        /// </summary>
        public ObjectTypeENUM ObjectType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalized bounding box (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public BoundingBoxSDTO BoundingBox { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Shape object (if ObjectType == Shape)
        /// </summary>
        public ShapeDTO Shape { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Chart object (if ObjectType == Chart)
        /// </summary>
        public ChartDTO Chart { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// SmartArt object (if ObjectType == SmartArt)
        /// </summary>
        public SmartArtDTO SmartArt { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Group object (if ObjectType == Group)
        /// </summary>
        public GroupDTO Group { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Placeholder object (if ObjectType == Placeholder)
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderDTO Placeholder { get; set; }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Metadata;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Placeholder

{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// PLACEHOLDER DTO
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Represents a PowerPoint placeholder (title, body, picture, chart, SmartArt, etc.)
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - RawPlaceholder contains raw extraction data.
    ///     - PlaceholderMetadataDTO contains semantic metadata (type, index, purpose).
    ///     - GeometryData contains the raw bounding box footprint.
    ///
    /// MVP PHILOSOPHY:
    ///     - Keep placeholders simple.
    ///     - Do NOT attempt to interpret content (that belongs to ObjectDTO or ShapeDTO).
    ///     - This DTO is strictly the placeholder container.
    ///
    /// USED BY:
    ///     - ExtractionToDTOMapper
    ///     - IssueDetector (placeholder overlap, off-slide)
    ///     - LayoutEngine (future)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class PlaceholderDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The placeholder type (Title, Body, Picture, Chart, SmartArt, etc.)
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderTypeENUM PlaceholderType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The placeholder index assigned by PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public int PlaceholderIndex { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw geometry footprint extracted directly from PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData RawGeometry { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Semantic metadata describing the placeholder's role and behavior.
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderMetadataDTO Metadata { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder is part of a group.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsInGroup { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The group ID if the placeholder belongs to a group.
        /// </summary>
        public int GroupId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalized bounding box (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public BoundingBoxSDTO BoundingBox { get; set; }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Presentation

{
    /// <summary>
    /// DistributionDTO represents how a slide, object, or deck has been
    /// distributed across systems, users, or external destinations.
    ///
    /// Consumed by:
    /// - SMARTshare (primary consumer)
    /// - SMARTmanage (distribution logs + workflow)
    /// - SMARTcomply (ensuring compliant content is distributed)
    ///

    /// - Populated exclusively by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.

        /// - Teams

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// MetadataDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents presentation-level metadata and custom properties.
//   This DTO captures authoring information, timestamps, tags,
//   custom document properties, theme inheritance metadata,
//   broadcast metadata, compliance metadata, and extraction metadata.
//
//   NEW ADDITION:
//     HasTemplateComplianceDirectives — a dormant, future-ready receptor
//     that will allow SMARTcomply (template-authoring mode) to embed
//     template-level compliance directives for downstream modules.
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added as SMARTtools expands.
//   - Consumed by SMARTcomply, SMARTaudit, SMARTshare, SMARTcast,
//     SMARTmanage, and the ExtractionToDTOMapper pipeline.

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Presentation
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO representing presentation-level metadata.
    /// </summary>
    public class MetadataDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // AUTHORING INFORMATION
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Name of the document author.
        /// </summary>
        public string Author { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Name of the last person to modify the document.
        /// </summary>
        public string LastModifiedBy { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Creation timestamp (string format for C# 7.3 compatibility).
        /// </summary>
        public string CreatedOn { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Last modification timestamp (string format for C# 7.3 compatibility).
        /// </summary>
        public string ModifiedOn { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // CUSTOM PROPERTIES
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Custom document properties (key/value pairs).
        /// </summary>
        public Dictionary<string, string> CustomProperties { get; set; }
            = new Dictionary<string, string>();

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // TAGS / LABELS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// User-facing tags or labels associated with the presentation.
        /// These are free-form labels used for classification, search,
        /// filtering, and grouping (e.g., "Training", "Draft", "Confidential").
        /// Internal semantic or compliance flags should NOT be stored here.
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> Tags { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // TEMPLATE COMPLIANCE DIRECTIVES (FUTURE-READY RECEPTOR)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Reserved for future use. Indicates whether the template contains
        /// author-defined compliance directives intended for SMARTcomply.
        /// Currently inactive until SMARTcomply template-authoring features
        /// are implemented. This receptor allows future modules to detect
        /// template-level compliance metadata without altering the DTO contract.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasTemplateComplianceDirectives { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // THEME / INHERITANCE METADATA
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Name of the applied theme (if any).
        /// </summary>
        public string ThemeName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// ID of the master slide used by the presentation.
        /// </summary>
        public string MasterSlideId { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BROADCAST METADATA
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the presentation is optimized for broadcast.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsBroadcastOptimized { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional broadcast notes or metadata.
        /// </summary>
        public string BroadcastNotes { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // COMPLIANCE METADATA
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the presentation has compliance issues.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasComplianceIssues { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance summary for presentation-level issues.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceSummary { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional semantic category (e.g., Training, Sales, Technical).
        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional analytics score indicating clarity or structure.
        /// </summary>
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional free-form analytics notes.
        /// </summary>
        public string AnalyticsNotes { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // EXTRACTION METADATA
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Version of the SMARTtools extractor used.
        /// </summary>
        public string ExtractorVersion { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Timestamp of extraction (string format for C# 7.3 compatibility).
        /// </summary>
        public string ExtractedOn { get; set; }
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// ShapeDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents a generic shape object extracted from a PowerPoint slide.
//   Shapes include rectangles, text boxes, callouts, arrows, icons,
//   SmartArt nodes, and any non-media visual element.
//
//   ShapeDTO is one of the largest DTOs in SMARTtools because shapes
//   carry text, formatting, geometry, compliance metadata, and semantic
//   meaning.
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added as SMARTtools expands.
//   - Consumed by SMARTcomply, SMARTshare, SMARTcast, SMARTmanage,
//     and the ExtractionToDTOMapper pipeline.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Shape
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO representing a generic shape on a slide.
    /// </summary>
    public class ShapeDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BASIC IDENTITY
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Unique identifier for the shape (internal or GUID).
        /// </summary>
        public string ShapeId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// PowerPoint shape type (e.g., Rectangle, TextBox, Callout, Arrow).
        /// </summary>
        public string ShapeType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this shape contains text.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw text content (if any).
        /// </summary>
        public string RawText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Sanitized text content (normalized for compliance/analytics).
        /// </summary>
        public string SanitizedText { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // DIMENSIONS / POSITION
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Width of the shape in points.
        /// </summary>
        public double Width { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Height of the shape in points.
        /// </summary>
        public double Height { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// X-position of the shape on the slide (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double PositionX { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Y-position of the shape on the slide (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double PositionY { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // GEOMETRY / TRANSFORM
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Rotation angle in degrees (0–360).
        /// </summary>
        public double Rotation { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the shape is flipped horizontally.
        /// </summary>
        public bool FlipHorizontal { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the shape is flipped vertically.
        /// </summary>
        public bool FlipVertical { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Z-order stacking position (higher = closer to front).
        /// </summary>
        public int ZOrder { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // FILL / LINE / FORMATTING
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Fill color (hex or theme reference).
        /// </summary>
        public string FillColor { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Line color (hex or theme reference).
        /// </summary>
        public string LineColor { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Line thickness (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double LineThickness { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the shape has a shadow effect.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasShadow { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the shape has a glow effect.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasGlow { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the shape has a reflection effect.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasReflection { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // TEXT FORMATTING (if applicable)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Font name used inside the shape.
        /// </summary>
        public string FontName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Font size used inside the shape.
        /// </summary>
        public double FontSize { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Text color (hex or theme reference).
        /// </summary>
        public string TextColor { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Text alignment (Left, Center, Right, Justify).
        /// </summary>
        public string TextAlignment { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // COMPLIANCE HOOKS (SMARTcomply)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this shape has compliance issues
        /// (e.g., low contrast, small text, misalignment).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasComplianceIssues { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance summary for shape issues.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceSummary { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BROADCAST / ROUTING (SMARTshare / SMARTcast)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this shape is relevant for broadcast scenarios.
        /// </summary>
        public bool RelevantForBroadcast { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional broadcast tag (e.g., "Title", "Callout", "KeyPoint").
        /// </summary>
        public string BroadcastTag { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional semantic category (e.g., "Title", "BodyText", "Annotation").
        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional analytics score indicating clarity or importance.
        /// </summary>
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional free-form analytics notes.
        /// </summary>
        public string AnalyticsNotes { get; set; }
    }
}

//

//     and the ExtractionToDTOMapper pipeline.

        /// <summary>
        /// Contrast adjustment (range typically -1.0 to +1.0).
        /// </summary>
        public double Contrast { get; set; }

        /// Transparency level (0 = opaque, 1 = fully transparent).

        public double Transparency { get; set; }

        /// <summary>

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public bool HasReflection { get; set; }

        /// <summary>

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// ShapeGeometryDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents geometry-specific metadata for a shape. PowerPoint shapes
//   vary widely in structure, so this DTO captures optional geometry
//   properties that apply only to certain shape types.
//
//   Examples:
//     - Rounded rectangles: corner radius
//     - Arrows: arrowhead types and sizes
//     - Callouts: callout angle and pointer position
//     - Connectors: routing type and connection points
//     - SmartArt nodes: node-level geometry metadata
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - All properties are optional; extraction sets only what applies.
//   - Consumed by SMARTcomply, SMARTshare, SMARTcast, SMARTmanage,
//     and the ExtractionToDTOMapper pipeline.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Shape
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO describing geometry-specific metadata for a shape.
    /// </summary>
    public class ShapeGeometryDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // ROUNDED RECTANGLES
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Corner radius (in points). Applies only to rounded rectangles.
        /// </summary>
        public double? CornerRadius { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // ARROWS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Arrowhead type at the start (e.g., None, Triangle, Stealth).
        /// </summary>
        public string ArrowStartType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Arrowhead type at the end (e.g., None, Triangle, Stealth).
        /// </summary>
        public string ArrowEndType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Arrowhead width at the start (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double? ArrowStartWidth { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Arrowhead width at the end (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double? ArrowEndWidth { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Arrowhead length at the start (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double? ArrowStartLength { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Arrowhead length at the end (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double? ArrowEndLength { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // CALLOUTS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Callout angle or pointer direction (if applicable).
        /// </summary>
        public double? CalloutAngle { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// X-position of the callout pointer (relative to shape).
        /// </summary>
        public double? CalloutPointerX { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Y-position of the callout pointer (relative to shape).
        /// </summary>
        public double? CalloutPointerY { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // CONNECTORS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Connector routing type (Straight, Elbow, Curved).
        /// </summary>
        public string ConnectorType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// ID of the shape this connector starts from (if any).
        /// </summary>
        public string ConnectorStartShapeId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// ID of the shape this connector ends at (if any).
        /// </summary>
        public string ConnectorEndShapeId { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SMARTART NODES
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// SmartArt node level (0 = root, 1 = child, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public int? SmartArtNodeLevel { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// SmartArt node index within its level.
        /// </summary>
        public int? SmartArtNodeIndex { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// SmartArt layout type (e.g., Process, Hierarchy, Cycle).
        /// </summary>
        public string SmartArtLayoutType { get; set; }
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// TextDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents text content within a slide object, including:
//   - Raw and sanitized text
//   - Font and formatting metadata
//   - Contrast and accessibility metadata
//   - Broadcast relevance
//   - Compliance hooks
//   - Semantic and analytics metadata
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - This DTO is a STABLE CONTRACT: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added in the future as needed.
//   - Consumed by ObjectDTO, SlideDTO, SMARTcomply, SMARTshare,
//     SMARTcast, SMARTmanage, and future modules.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Shape
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Data transfer object representing text content within a slide object.
    /// </summary>
    public class TextDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // RAW / SANITIZED TEXT
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw text exactly as extracted from the object.
        /// </summary>
        public string RawText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Sanitized text (normalized whitespace, cleaned formatting).
        /// </summary>
        public string SanitizedText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the text is empty or whitespace-only.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsEmpty { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // FONT / FORMATTING METADATA
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Font family used for the text.
        /// </summary>
        public string FontFamily { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Font size in points.
        /// </summary>
        public double? FontSize { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the text is bold.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsBold { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the text is italic.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsItalic { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the text is underlined.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsUnderlined { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Text color (hex or named).
        /// </summary>
        public string TextColor { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Background color behind the text (if applicable).
        /// </summary>
        public string BackgroundColor { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional alignment descriptor (e.g., "Left", "Center", "Right", "Justified").
        /// </summary>
        public string Alignment { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // ACCESSIBILITY / CONTRAST (SMARTcomply)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the text meets WCAG contrast guidelines.
        /// </summary>
        public bool MeetsContrastGuidelines { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional contrast ratio (e.g., 4.5 for WCAG AA).
        /// </summary>
        public double? ContrastRatio { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the text may be difficult to read (e.g., small font).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasReadabilityIssues { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance summary for text issues.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceSummary { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BROADCAST / ROUTING (SMARTshare / SMARTcast)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this text is relevant for broadcast scenarios
        /// (e.g., captions, overlays, narration cues).
        /// </summary>
        public bool RelevantForBroadcast { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional broadcast tag (e.g., "Caption", "Overlay", "NarrationCue").
        /// </summary>
        public string BroadcastTag { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional semantic category (e.g., "Header", "Body", "KeyPoint", "Footnote").
        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional analytics score indicating clarity or importance.
        /// </summary>
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional free-form analytics notes.
        /// </summary>
        public string AnalyticsNotes { get; set; }
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// TextFormatDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents detailed formatting metadata for text content, including:
//   - Paragraph-level formatting (line spacing, paragraph spacing)
//   - Bullet and numbering metadata
//   - Indentation and alignment
//   - Compliance and broadcast hooks
//   - Semantic and analytics metadata
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - This DTO is a STABLE CONTRACT: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added in the future as needed.
//   - Consumed by TextDTO, ObjectDTO, SMARTcomply, SMARTshare,
//     SMARTcast, SMARTmanage, and future modules.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Shape
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Data transfer object representing detailed text formatting metadata.
    /// </summary>
    public class TextFormatDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // PARAGRAPH / LINE SPACING
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Line spacing multiplier (e.g., 1.0, 1.15, 1.5).
        /// </summary>
        public double? LineSpacing { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Space before the paragraph (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double? SpaceBefore { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Space after the paragraph (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double? SpaceAfter { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // INDENTATION
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Left indentation (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double? IndentLeft { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Right indentation (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double? IndentRight { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// First-line indentation (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double? IndentFirstLine { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // ALIGNMENT
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Paragraph alignment (e.g., "Left", "Center", "Right", "Justified").
        /// </summary>
        public string Alignment { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BULLETS / NUMBERING
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the paragraph uses bullets.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsBulleted { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Bullet character or symbol (if applicable).
        /// </summary>
        public string BulletCharacter { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Bullet level (e.g., 0 for top-level, 1 for nested).
        /// </summary>
        public int? BulletLevel { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the paragraph uses numbering.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsNumbered { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Numbering style (e.g., "1.", "a.", "i.").
        /// </summary>
        public string NumberingStyle { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Numbering level (e.g., 0 for top-level, 1 for nested).
        /// </summary>
        public int? NumberingLevel { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // COMPLIANCE HOOKS (SMARTcomply)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the formatting may cause compliance issues
        /// (e.g., excessive indentation, inconsistent bullet levels).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasComplianceIssues { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance summary for formatting issues.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceSummary { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BROADCAST / ROUTING (SMARTshare / SMARTcast)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this formatting is relevant for broadcast scenarios
        /// (e.g., caption layout, overlay spacing).
        /// </summary>
        public bool RelevantForBroadcast { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional broadcast tag (e.g., "CaptionFormat", "OverlayFormat").
        /// </summary>
        public string BroadcastTag { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional semantic category (e.g., "List", "Body", "Header").
        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional analytics score indicating formatting clarity or consistency.
        /// </summary>
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional free-form analytics notes.
        /// </summary>
        public string AnalyticsNotes { get; set; }
    }

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// AnimationDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents a single animation applied to an object on a slide.
//   Animations influence broadcast sequencing, compliance timing,
//   semantic emphasis, accessibility, and SMARTcast narration alignment.
//
//   This DTO captures the essential metadata needed for downstream
//   SMARTtools modules to understand and evaluate animation behavior.
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added as SMARTtools expands.
//   - Consumed by SMARTcomply, SMARTshare, SMARTcast, SMARTmanage,
//     and the ExtractionToDTOMapper pipeline.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Slide
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO representing a single animation applied to a slide object.
    /// </summary>
    public class AnimationDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BASIC IDENTITY
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Unique identifier for the animation (internal or GUID).
        /// </summary>
        public string AnimationId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// ID of the object this animation targets (Shape, Image, Chart, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public string TargetObjectId { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // ANIMATION TYPE & BEHAVIOR
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Animation type (e.g., Fade, FlyIn, Wipe, Zoom, GrowShrink).
        /// </summary>
        public string AnimationType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Trigger type (OnClick, WithPrevious, AfterPrevious).
        /// </summary>
        public string TriggerType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Duration of the animation (in seconds).
        /// </summary>
        public double? Duration { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Delay before the animation begins (in seconds).
        /// </summary>
        public double? Delay { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // EFFECT PARAMETERS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Direction of the animation (e.g., Left, Right, Up, Down).
        /// Applies only to directional animations.
        /// </summary>
        public string Direction { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional intensity or magnitude (e.g., for Grow/Shrink).
        /// </summary>
        public double? Intensity { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // COMPLIANCE HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this animation has compliance issues
        /// (e.g., too fast, too distracting, accessibility concerns).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasComplianceIssues { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance summary for animation-level issues.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceSummary { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional semantic role (e.g., Emphasis, Entrance, Exit).
        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticRole { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional analytics score indicating clarity or importance.
        /// </summary>
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional free-form analytics notes.
        /// </summary>
        public string AnalyticsNotes { get; set; }
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// LayoutDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents the layout metadata for a slide, including:
//   - Layout identity (name, type)
//   - Placeholder structure
//   - Geometry hints
//   - Theme alignment
//   - Compliance and semantic hooks
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - This DTO is a STABLE CONTRACT: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added in the future as needed.
//   - Consumed by SlideDTO, ExtractionToDTOMapper, SMARTcomply, SMARTshare,
//     SMARTmanage, and future modules.

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Slide
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Data transfer object representing the layout applied to a slide.
    /// </summary>
    public class LayoutDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BASIC IDENTITY
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// The name of the layout as defined in the source deck.
        /// </summary>
        public string LayoutName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Logical layout type (e.g., TitleOnly, TitleAndContent, TwoContent).
        /// </summary>
        public string LayoutType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional unique identifier for the layout.
        /// </summary>
        public string LayoutId { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // PLACEHOLDER STRUCTURE
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of placeholders defined in this layout.
        /// </summary>
        public int PlaceholderCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the layout includes a title placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasTitlePlaceholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the layout includes a content placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasContentPlaceholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the layout includes a secondary content placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasSecondaryContentPlaceholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the layout includes a footer placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasFooterPlaceholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the layout includes a date/time placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasDatePlaceholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the layout includes a slide number placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasSlideNumberPlaceholder { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // GEOMETRY / STRUCTURE
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional geometry descriptor (e.g., "Balanced", "Dense", "Sparse").
        /// </summary>
        public string GeometryDescriptor { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional layout density score (0–1), used for analytics.
        /// </summary>
        public double? DensityScore { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // THEME ALIGNMENT
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Name of the theme associated with this layout.
        /// </summary>
        public string ThemeName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional theme variant (e.g., "Dark", "Light", "Accent1").
        /// </summary>
        public string ThemeVariant { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // COMPLIANCE HOOKS (SMARTcomply)
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this layout is known to cause compliance issues
        /// (e.g., low contrast, overloaded content areas).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasKnownComplianceRisks { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance notes for this layout.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceNotes { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional semantic category for this layout (e.g., "Title", "Agenda").
        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional analytics score indicating layout quality or suitability.
        /// </summary>
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Metadata;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Slide
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// SLIDE DTO
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Represents a single PowerPoint slide containing objects, metadata, and geometry.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - RawSlideData contains raw extraction values.
    ///     - SlideDTO provides semantic meaning and structure for IssueDetector and LayoutEngine.
    ///
    /// MVP PHILOSOPHY:
    ///     - Keep slide wrapper simple.
    ///     - Only include objects, geometry, and metadata.
    ///
    /// USED BY:
    ///     - ExtractionToDTOMapper
    ///     - PresentationDTO
    ///     - IssueDetector (slide-level analysis)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class SlideDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The slide number (1-based index).
        /// </summary>
        public int SlideNumber { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw geometry footprint extracted directly from PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData RawGeometry { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Semantic metadata describing the slide (layout type, title presence, etc.)
        /// </summary>
        public SlideMetadata Metadata { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalized bounding box (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public BoundingBoxSDTO BoundingBox { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// All objects on the slide (shapes, charts, SmartArt, groups, placeholders).
        /// </summary>
        public List<ObjectDTO> Objects { get; set; } = new List<ObjectDTO>();
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO

// PURPOSE:
//   Represents the theme metadata applied to a slide or deck, including:

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Slide
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Data transfer object representing a theme applied to a slide or deck.
    /// </summary>
    public class ThemeDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BASIC IDENTITY
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Name of the theme (e.g., "Office", "CustomBrandTheme").
        /// </summary>
        public string ThemeName { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional theme variant (e.g., "Dark", "Light", "Accent1").

        public string ThemeVariant { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional unique identifier for the theme.
        /// </summary>
        public string ThemeId { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// </summary>

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public string AccentColor2 { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Background color.
        /// </summary>

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>

        /// </summary>

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public string TitleFontFamily { get; set; }

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>
        public double? BodyFontSize { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional font size baseline for titles.
        /// </summary>
        public double? TitleFontSize { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BACKGROUND STYLE
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Background style descriptor (e.g., "Solid", "Gradient", "Image").
        /// </summary>
        public string BackgroundStyle { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional background image URI or identifier.
        /// </summary>
        public string BackgroundImage { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BRANDING / COMPLIANCE HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this theme is part of a corporate brand package.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsCorporateBrandTheme { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional brand identifier (e.g., "Contoso", "Fabrikam").
        /// </summary>
        public string BrandId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this theme meets accessibility contrast guidelines.
        /// </summary>
        public bool MeetsContrastGuidelines { get; set; }

        /// <summary>

        public string ComplianceNotes { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }

        /// </summary>

    }

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// TransitionDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents the transition metadata applied to a slide, including:
//   - Transition identity (name, type)
//   - Duration and timing
//   - Trigger behavior (OnClick, AfterPrevious, AutoAdvance)
//   - Auto?advance timing
//   - Media blocking behavior
//   - Compliance and semantic hooks
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - New properties may be added as SMARTtools expands.
//   - Consumed by SlideDTO, SMARTcast, SMARTshare, SMARTcomply,
//     SMARTmanage, and future modules.

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Slide
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Data transfer object representing a slide transition.
    /// </summary>
    public class TransitionDTO
    {
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // BASIC IDENTITY
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Transition type (Fade, Push, Wipe, Morph, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public string TransitionType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional category (Subtle, Exciting, Dynamic).
        /// </summary>
        public string TransitionCategory { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // TIMING / DURATION
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Duration of the transition (in seconds).
        /// </summary>
        public double? DurationSeconds { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Trigger type: OnClick, AfterPrevious, AutoAdvance.
        /// </summary>
        public string TriggerType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Auto?advance timing (in seconds), only applies when TriggerType = "AutoAdvance".
        /// </summary>
        public double? AutoAdvanceAfter { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // MEDIA BLOCKING BEHAVIOR
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether media on the slide can delay the transition
        /// to the next slide (e.g., auto?playing videos).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasBlockingMedia { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // COMPLIANCE HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether this transition may cause accessibility issues
        /// (e.g., excessive motion).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasComplianceRisks { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional compliance notes.
        /// </summary>
        public string ComplianceNotes { get; set; }

        // --------------------------------------------------------------------
        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS HOOKS
        // --------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional semantic category (e.g., Calm, Energetic).
        /// </summary>
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional analytics score indicating transition quality or suitability.
        /// </summary>
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;


namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.SmartArt
{
    /// <summary>
    /// SmartArtDTO represents a SmartArt object extracted from a PowerPoint slide.
    /// SmartArt is hierarchical, so this DTO contains a tree of SmartArtNodeDTO
    /// objects. Each node may contain text, geometry, and child nodes.
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    ///     - Pure data container (no logic).
    ///     - Populated by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    ///     - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// </summary>
    public class SmartArtDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The SmartArt layout type (e.g., Process, Hierarchy, Cycle).
        /// </summary>
        public SmartArtLayoutENUM LayoutType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The root node of the SmartArt hierarchy.
        /// </summary>
        public SmartArtNodeDTO RootNode { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the SmartArt object is part of a group.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsInGroup { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The group ID if the SmartArt belongs to a group.
        /// </summary>
        public int GroupId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw geometry footprint extracted directly from PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData RawGeometry { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalized bounding box (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public BoundingBoxSDTO BoundingBox { get; set; }
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// TableBorderDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents all border thickness values for a table, including:
//   - Outer borders (top, bottom, left, right)
//   - Internal borders (horizontal and vertical gridlines)
//   This DTO allows SMARTcomply, SMARTshare, and SMARTmanage to evaluate
//   table readability, contrast, and broadcast suitability.
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - Thickness values are expressed in points (PowerPoint native units).

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Table
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO describing border thickness for a table.
    /// </summary>
    public class TableBorderDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Thickness of the top outer border (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double Top { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Thickness of the bottom outer border (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double Bottom { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Thickness of the left outer border (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double Left { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Thickness of the right outer border (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double Right { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Thickness of internal horizontal gridlines (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double HorizontalInternal { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Thickness of internal vertical gridlines (in points).
        /// </summary>
        public double VerticalInternal { get; set; }
    }
}

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Table
{
    public class TableCellDTO
    {
        public int RowIndex { get; set; }
        public int ColumnIndex { get; set; }

        // CONTENT
        public string RawText { get; set; }
        public string SanitizedText { get; set; }
        public bool IsEmpty { get; set; }

        // MERGE METADATA
        public bool IsMergedCell { get; set; }
        public int? MergeRowSpan { get; set; }
        public int? MergeColumnSpan { get; set; }

        // FORMATTING
        public string BackgroundColor { get; set; }
        public string TextColor { get; set; }
        public string Alignment { get; set; }

        // COMPLIANCE
        public bool HasComplianceIssues { get; set; }
        public string ComplianceSummary { get; set; }

        // BROADCAST
        public bool RelevantForBroadcast { get; set; }
        public string BroadcastTag { get; set; }

        // SEMANTIC / ANALYTICS
        public string SemanticCategory { get; set; }
        public double? AnalyticsScore { get; set; }
        public string AnalyticsNotes { get; set; }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Table
{
    /// <summary>
    /// TableDTO represents a PowerPoint table object.
    /// Contains row/column structure, cell metadata, merged cell info,
    /// geometry, and style information.
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// - Pure data container (no logic).
    /// - Populated by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
    /// - Stable contract: fields may be added, but never removed or renamed.
    /// </summary>
    public class TableDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Number of rows in the table.
        /// </summary>
        public int RowCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of columns in the table.
        /// </summary>
        public int ColumnCount { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// All table cells in row-major order.
        /// </summary>
        public List<TableCellDTO> Cells { get; set; } = new List<TableCellDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Merged cell metadata (row spans, column spans).
        /// </summary>
        public List<TableMergeDTO> Merges { get; set; } = new List<TableMergeDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Table border metadata (outer + inner borders).
        /// </summary>
        public TableBorderDTO Borders { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Table style metadata (theme, shading, banding).
        /// </summary>
        public TableStyleDTO Style { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Geometry footprint for the entire table.
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryData Geometry { get; set; }
    }
}

// SMARTtools.Core.DTO
// TableMergeDTO.cs
//
// PURPOSE:
//   Represents a merged cell region within a table, including:
//   - Starting row/column
//   - Row span and column span
//   This DTO allows SMARTcomply to detect merged cells, evaluate
//   accessibility issues, and ensure broadcast clarity.
//
// CONTRACT NOTES:
//   - Stable DTO contract: do not remove or rename existing properties.
//   - Merge spans follow PowerPoint's native indexing (zero-based).

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Table
{
    /// <summary>
    /// DTO describing a merged cell region within a table.
    /// </summary>
    public class TableMergeDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Zero-based row index where the merged region begins.
        /// </summary>
        public int StartRow { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Zero-based column index where the merged region begins.
        /// </summary>
        public int StartColumn { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of rows included in the merged region.
        /// </summary>
        public int RowSpan { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Number of columns included in the merged region.
        /// </summary>
        public int ColumnSpan { get; set; }
    }
}
