using System;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Data
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents raw text data extracted from a PowerPoint shape.
    /// 
    /// ROLE IN ARCHITECTURE:
    /// This is a RAW EXTRACTION MODEL for text content and formatting.
    /// It captures text exactly as it exists in PowerPoint before normalization.
    /// 
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    /// Text is fundamental to presentations. We need to extract:
    /// - The actual text content (for compliance, accessibility, search)
    /// - Font information (for device optimization, brand compliance)
    /// - Formatting (for accurate device-specific rendering)
    /// 
    /// DATA FLOW:
    /// PowerPoint TextFrame ? TextData ? TextNormalizationBuilder ? 
    /// TextMapper ? TextDTO
    /// 
    /// USED BY:
    /// - PowerPointInteropWrapper (populates this)
    /// - TextNormalizationBuilder (normalizes text content)
    /// - TextMapper (maps to TextDTO)
    /// - IssueDetector (detects empty text, overflow issues)
    /// 
    /// CRITICAL FOR:
    /// - Device optimization (font substitution on different platforms)
    /// - Accessibility compliance (contrast, readability)
    /// - Brand compliance (font/color validation)
    /// - Copilot integration (content analysis, suggestions)
    /// </summary>
    public class TextData
    {
        // =====================================================================
        // IDENTITY & LOCATION
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the shape ID that contains this text.
        /// 
        /// WHY WE NEED THIS:
        /// Text is always contained in a shape. We need to cross-reference
        /// TextData back to ShapeData for geometry calculations and overlap detection.
        /// </summary>
        public int ShapeId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the 1-based slide index this text appears on.
        /// 
        /// WHY WE STORE THIS:
        /// Diagnostics need to report "Text issue on slide 5, shape 12".
        /// Denormalizing SlideIndex here avoids joins during issue reporting.
        /// </summary>
        public int SlideIndex { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // TEXT CONTENT
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the raw text content (unmodified from PowerPoint).
        /// 
        /// WHY "RAW":
        /// This is the text EXACTLY as extracted from PowerPoint, including:
        /// - Extra whitespace
        /// - Line breaks (may be \r\n, \n, or PowerPoint's internal breaks)
        /// - Special characters
        /// - Zero-width spaces (PowerPoint quirk)
        /// 
        /// TextNormalizationBuilder will clean this later. We preserve the raw
        /// text here so we can detect what PowerPoint actually contains vs what
        /// we normalized it to (useful for diagnostics).
        /// </summary>
        public string RawText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets whether the text is empty or whitespace-only.
        /// 
        /// WHY WE FLAG THIS:
        /// Empty text shapes are a common issue (user forgot to fill placeholder).
        /// IssueDetector uses this flag to report empty text without re-parsing.
        /// 
        /// PERFORMANCE NOTE:
        /// We set this flag during extraction (cheap) rather than re-checking
        /// during diagnostics (expensive - thousands of shapes).
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsEmpty { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // FONT PROPERTIES (CRITICAL FOR DEVICE OPTIMIZATION)
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the font family name (e.g., "Calibri", "Arial").
        /// 
        /// WHY THIS IS CRITICAL:
        /// Font availability varies by platform:
        /// - Windows has "Segoe UI"
        /// - Mac has "SF Pro"
        /// - Linux has "Liberation Sans"
        /// 
        /// Device optimization (SMARTshare) uses font family to determine if
        /// substitution is needed for the target device.
        /// 
        /// POWERPOINT QUIRK:
        /// PowerPoint may return "+Body" or "+Headings" for theme fonts.
        /// We store this as-is and resolve to actual font in mapping stage.
        /// </summary>
        public string FontFamily { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the font size in points.
        /// 
        /// WHY POINTS:
        /// PowerPoint uses points (1/72 inch) for font sizing.
        /// We preserve this unit for consistency with PowerPoint.
        /// 
        /// WHY THIS MATTERS:
        /// - Small fonts (<18pt) may be unreadable on video calls (SMARTcast)
        /// - Font size affects text box height calculations (overflow detection)
        /// - Device optimization may scale font size for different screen densities
        /// </summary>
        public float FontSize { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the font weight (normal, bold, etc.).
        /// 
        /// WHY ENUM:
        /// PowerPoint exposes Bold as boolean, but fonts can have multiple weights
        /// (Light, Regular, Medium, SemiBold, Bold, ExtraBold).
        /// FontWeightENUM normalizes this for consistent handling.
        /// </summary>
        public FontWeightENUM FontWeight { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets whether the text is italic.
        /// 
        /// WHY BOOLEAN:
        /// Italic is binary in PowerPoint (on or off).
        /// 
        /// WHY WE TRACK THIS:
        /// Font substitution on different devices must preserve italic styling.
        /// Not all fonts have italic variants (device optimization challenge).
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsItalic { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets whether the text is underlined.
        /// 
        /// WHY WE TRACK THIS:
        /// Accessibility: Underlined text should not be the only indicator of links.
        /// Device optimization: Underline rendering varies by platform.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsUnderlined { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // COLOR PROPERTIES (CRITICAL FOR ACCESSIBILITY & BRANDING)
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the text color in hex format (#RRGGBB or #AARRGGBB).
        /// 
        /// WHY HEX:
        /// Hex is platform-agnostic and easily comparable for brand compliance.
        /// 
        /// WHY THIS IS CRITICAL:
        /// - Accessibility: Contrast ratio between text and background
        /// - Brand compliance: Corporate colors must match exactly
        /// - Device optimization: Color space conversion (sRGB vs Display P3 on Mac)
        /// 
        /// POWERPOINT QUIRK:
        /// PowerPoint may return RGB as int or theme color name.
        /// Extraction converts everything to hex for consistency.
        /// </summary>
        public string TextColor { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the background/highlight color (if any).
        /// 
        /// WHY WE NEED THIS:
        /// Contrast calculations require both foreground AND background color.
        /// Accessibility compliance (WCAG) validates contrast ratios.
        /// 
        /// NULL IF NO BACKGROUND:
        /// Most text has transparent background. We store null to distinguish
        /// "no background" from "white background" (different for contrast checks).
        /// </summary>
        public string BackgroundColor { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // ALIGNMENT & LAYOUT
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the text alignment (left, center, right, justified).
        /// 
        /// WHY WE TRACK THIS:
        /// Device optimization must preserve alignment intent.
        /// Different screen widths may cause text reflow, but alignment
        /// should remain consistent.
        /// </summary>
        public TextAlignmentENUM Alignment { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the line spacing multiplier (1.0 = single space).
        /// 
        /// WHY THIS MATTERS:
        /// Line spacing affects text box height calculations.
        /// Overflow detection must account for line spacing.
        /// Device optimization may adjust line spacing for readability on small screens.
        /// </summary>
        public float LineSpacing { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // TEXT BOX PROPERTIES
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets whether the text box auto-fits text (shrink on overflow).
        /// 
        /// WHY THIS IS IMPORTANT:
        /// PowerPoint can auto-shrink text to fit the shape.
        /// Device optimization needs to know if auto-fit is enabled to decide
        /// whether to resize shape or shrink font on target device.
        /// 
        /// POWERPOINT QUIRK:
        /// Auto-fit behavior is unreliable across platforms. SMARTshare may
        /// disable auto-fit and handle sizing explicitly for predictability.
        /// </summary>
        public bool AutoFit { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets whether the text is wrapping within the shape.
        /// 
        /// WHY WE NEED THIS:
        /// Non-wrapping text can overflow shape boundaries.
        /// Overflow detection logic differs for wrapping vs non-wrapping text.
        /// </summary>
        public bool WordWrap { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the internal margin/padding inside the text box (in points).
        /// 
        /// WHY THIS MATTERS:
        /// Text box margins affect available text space.
        /// Overflow calculations must subtract margins from shape width/height.
        /// 
        /// POWERPOINT QUIRK:
        /// PowerPoint has separate margins for top/bottom/left/right.
        /// For simplicity in extraction, we store a single "average" margin.
        /// Detailed margin extraction happens in StructuralDTO layer if needed.
        /// </summary>
        public float MarginPoints { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // PLACEHOLDER CONTEXT
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets whether this text is in a placeholder shape.
        /// 
        /// WHY THIS MATTERS:
        /// Placeholder text has special significance:
        /// - Title placeholders: Accessibility requires titles
        /// - Content placeholders: Template enforcement validates usage
        /// - Footer placeholders: Brand compliance may require specific text
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsPlaceholderText { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the placeholder type (if IsPlaceholderText is true).
        /// 
        /// WHY WE NEED THE TYPE:
        /// Different placeholder types have different validation rules.
        /// Title text should never be empty. Footer text might be optional.
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderTypeENUM? PlaceholderType { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // RICH TEXT / RUNS (FUTURE EXPANSION)
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets whether this text has mixed formatting (multiple fonts/colors/sizes).
        /// 
        /// WHY WE FLAG THIS:
        /// Text with mixed formatting (called "runs" in PowerPoint) is complex:
        /// - "Welcome" might be Bold 24pt
        /// - " to " might be Regular 18pt
        /// - "SMARTtools" might be Italic 24pt with different color
        /// 
        /// Device optimization must preserve these formatting runs.
        /// 
        /// CURRENT STATE:
        /// For MVP, we extract "dominant" formatting (most common font/size/color).
        /// Future: Extract full run details into ShapeTextRunDTO.
        /// 
        /// WHY NOT NOW:
        /// Run extraction is complex and not critical for initial device optimization.
        /// We can add this later without breaking existing architecture (DTO universe expansion).
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasMixedFormatting { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // HYPERLINKS
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets whether this text contains hyperlinks.
        /// 
        /// WHY WE TRACK THIS:
        /// Hyperlinks complicate device optimization:
        /// - Links may not work on all devices (internal file paths)
        /// - Accessibility requires link text to be descriptive
        /// - Security: External links may need approval (SMARTmanage)
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasHyperlinks { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // METADATA
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Gets or sets the text extraction timestamp (when we read this from PowerPoint).
        /// 
        /// WHY TIMESTAMP:
        /// If extraction takes a long time (large deck), timestamps help debug
        /// whether extraction is slow or other stages are slow.
        /// 
        /// Also useful for audit trails (SMARTaudit future product).
        /// </summary>
        public DateTime ExtractedAt { get; set; }

        // =====================================================================
        // CONSTRUCTOR
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Initializes a new instance of the <see cref="TextData"/> class.
        /// 
        /// WHY DEFAULT CONSTRUCTOR:
        /// Extraction code populates properties incrementally as data is read
        /// from PowerPoint TextFrame object.
        /// </summary>
        public TextData()
        {
            // Set extraction timestamp immediately
            ExtractedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }
    }
}
namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Data
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// TEXT SHAPE DTO
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Represents a text-bearing shape extracted from a slide.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - The InteropWrapper extracts text from PowerPoint shapes.
    ///     - PowerPointContext stores normalized text data.
    ///     - IssueDetector uses this to detect empty text shapes.
    ///
    /// KEY FIELDS:
    ///     SlideIndex:
    ///         Identifies which slide the text shape belongs to.
    ///
    ///     ShapeId:
    ///         The unique PowerPoint shape ID (used for geometry and issue mapping).
    ///
    ///     Text:
    ///         The extracted text content.
    ///         Empty or whitespace-only text is flagged by IssueDetector.
    ///
    /// MVP PHILOSOPHY:
    ///     - Keep text extraction simple.
    ///     - Avoid font/style metadata until SMARTstyle module is introduced.
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class TextData
    {
        public int SlideIndex { get; set; }
        public int ShapeId { get; set; }

        // Legacy field required by IssueDetector + TextNormalizationBatch
        public string RawText { get; set; }

        // Your new field
        public string Text { get; set; }
    }

}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Geometry
{
    /// <summary>
    /// AnimationGeometryData represents the raw geometric footprint and metadata
    /// of ANY animation applied to a PowerPoint shape.
    ///
    /// This includes:
    /// - Entrance animations (Fade, Fly In, Zoom)
    /// - Exit animations (Fade Out, Fly Out)
    /// - Emphasis animations (Pulse, Spin, Grow/Shrink)
    /// - Motion paths
    /// - Timing and duration
    /// - Trigger information
    ///
    /// AnimationGeometryData contains ONLY raw information extracted from PowerPoint.
    /// No geometry math, no path interpretation, no timing normalization.
    ///
    /// The Engine layer (AnimationGeometryBuilder, IssueDetector, OGMBuilder)
    /// consumes AnimationGeometryData and produces higher-level animation geometry.
    ///
    /// AnimationGeometryData is intentionally minimal and stable — it should not change
    /// unless the PowerPoint animation object model itself changes.
    /// </summary>
    public class AnimationGeometryData
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// PowerPoint's internal animation ID.
        /// This is stable and used for mapping across the pipeline.
        /// </summary>
        public int AnimationId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The shape ID this animation is applied to.
        /// This links animation geometry directly to ShapeData.
        /// </summary>
        public int ShapeId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The high-level animation type (Entrance, Exit, Emphasis, MotionPath).
        /// This maps to our AnimationType enum for consistency across the Engine.
        /// </summary>
        public AnimationTypeENUM AnimationType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw duration of the animation in seconds.
        /// </summary>
        public double DurationSeconds { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw delay before the animation begins (in seconds).
        /// </summary>
        public double DelaySeconds { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the animation is triggered by a click.
        /// False if it is automatic or part of a sequence.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsTriggeredByClick { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the animation is part of a sequence (e.g., "After Previous").
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsSequential { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw motion path data (if applicable).
        /// Stored as a simple string — the Engine interprets it later.
        /// </summary>
        public string MotionPathRaw { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw position (Left) in EMUs at the start of the animation.
        /// </summary>
        public long StartX_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw position (Top) in EMUs at the start of the animation.
        /// </summary>
        public long StartY_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw position (Left) in EMUs at the end of the animation.
        /// </summary>
        public long EndX_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw position (Top) in EMUs at the end of the animation.
        /// </summary>
        public long EndY_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw OOXML for the animation element.
        /// Extremely useful for debugging and metadata extraction.
        /// </summary>
        public string RawOOXML { get; set; }
    }
}

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// BOUNDING BOX (RAW)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Represents the raw geometric bounds of any object or slide.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - Raw extraction needs a universal geometry primitive.
    ///     - DTOs depend on normalized geometry.
    ///     - LayoutEngine uses bounding boxes for alignment and spacing.
    ///
    /// MVP:
    ///     - No rotation.
    ///     - No transforms.
    ///     - No complex geometry.
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class BoundingBox
    {
        /// <summary>X coordinate of the top-left corner.</summary>
        public float X { get; set; }

        /// <summary>Y coordinate of the top-left corner.</summary>
        public float Y { get; set; }

        /// <summary>Width of the object.</summary>
        public float Width { get; set; }

        /// <summary>Height of the object.</summary>
        public float Height { get; set; }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Geometry

{
    /// <summary>
    /// PlaceholderGeometryData represents the raw geometric footprint and semantic
    /// metadata of ANY placeholder extracted from a PowerPoint slide.
    ///
    /// This includes:
    /// - Title placeholders
    /// - Subtitle placeholders
    /// - Body text placeholders
    /// - Picture placeholders
    /// - Chart placeholders
    /// - Table placeholders
    /// - Media placeholders
    /// - Custom layout placeholders
    ///
    /// PlaceholderGeometryData contains ONLY raw geometry + semantic placeholder type.
    /// No math, no normalization, no interpretation.
    ///
    /// The Engine layer (PlaceholderGeometryBuilder, IssueDetector, OGMBuilder)
    /// consumes PlaceholderGeometryData and produces higher-level semantic geometry.
    ///
    /// PlaceholderGeometryData is intentionally minimal and stable — it should not change
    /// unless the PowerPoint placeholder model itself changes.
    /// </summary>
    public class PlaceholderGeometryData
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// PowerPoint's internal shape ID for the placeholder.
        /// This links PlaceholderGeometryData directly to ShapeData.
        /// </summary>
        public int ShapeId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The semantic placeholder type (Title, Subtitle, Body, Picture, Chart, Table, etc.).
        /// This maps to our PlaceholderType enum for consistency across the Engine.
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderTypeENUM PlaceholderType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw position (Left) in EMUs.
        /// EMU = English Metric Unit (1 inch = 914,400 EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public long X_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw position (Top) in EMUs.
        /// </summary>
        public long Y_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw width in EMUs.
        /// </summary>
        public long Width_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw height in EMUs.
        /// </summary>
        public long Height_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Rotation in degrees (0–360).
        /// </summary>
        public double RotationDegrees { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder is positioned outside the visible slide bounds.
        /// Used by IssueDetector and layout analysis.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsOffSlide { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Distance from the slide center (in EMUs).
        /// Used for off-slide detection and spatial analysis.
        /// </summary>
        public long DistanceFromSlideCenter_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder is flipped horizontally.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsFlippedHorizontal { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder is flipped vertically.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsFlippedVertical { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw OOXML for the placeholder geometry element.
        /// Extremely useful for debugging and metadata extraction.
        /// </summary>
        public string RawOOXML { get; set; }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.MetaData
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Issue represents a single problem detected on a slide.
    ///
    /// Issues are produced by IssueDetector and consumed by:
    /// - OGMBuilder
    /// - SMARTshare payload builders
    /// - SMARTwriter (future)
    /// - SMARTlayout (future)
    ///
    /// Issue contains:
    /// - Issue type (enum)
    /// - Severity (enum)
    /// - Slide index
    /// - Shape/media/placeholder/text/animation references
    /// - Human-readable description
    ///
    /// Issue contains NO geometry, NO OOXML, NO heavy objects.
    /// It is intentionally minimal and stable.
    /// </summary>
    public class Issue
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The type of issue detected (MissingTitle, OffSlideObject, LowResolutionImage, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public IssueType IssueType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Severity of the issue (Info, Warning, Error).
        /// </summary>
        public IssueSeverityENUM Severity { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Slide index where the issue occurred.
        /// </summary>
        public int SlideIndex { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional shape ID associated with the issue.
        /// Null if the issue is slide-level.
        /// </summary>
        public int? ShapeId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional media ID associated with the issue.
        /// Null if the issue is not media-related.
        /// </summary>
        public int? MediaId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional animation ID associated with the issue.
        /// Null if the issue is not animation-related.
        /// </summary>
        public int? AnimationId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional placeholder type associated with the issue.
        /// Null if the issue is not placeholder-related.
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderTypeENUM? PlaceholderType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Human-readable description of the issue.
        /// This is what appears in SMARTshare and diagnostics.
        /// </summary>
        public string Description { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Optional raw context string for debugging.
        /// This may contain extracted metadata or diagnostic notes.
        /// </summary>
        public string Context { get; set; }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Metadata
{
    /// <summary>
    /// PlaceholderMetadataData represents the semantic meaning of ALL placeholders
    /// on a slide. This includes:
    /// - Title placeholders
    /// - Subtitle placeholders
    /// - Body text placeholders
    /// - Picture placeholders
    /// - Chart placeholders
    /// - Table placeholders
    /// - Media placeholders
    /// - Custom layout placeholders
    ///
    /// This DTO contains ONLY semantic metadata — no geometry.
    /// Geometry is handled separately by PlaceholderGeometryData.
    ///
    /// The Engine layer (PlaceholderMetadataBuilder, IssueDetector, OGMBuilder)
    /// consumes PlaceholderMetadataData to understand the intent and role of
    /// each placeholder in the slide layout.
    ///
    /// PlaceholderMetadataData is intentionally minimal and stable — it should not
    /// change unless the PowerPoint placeholder model itself changes.
    /// </summary>
    public class PlaceholderMetadataData
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Collection of semantic metadata entries for each placeholder on the slide.
        /// Each entry describes the placeholder's meaning, role, and state.
        /// </summary>
        public List<PlaceholderMetadata> Placeholders { get; set; }
            = new List<PlaceholderMetadata>();
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// PlaceholderMetadata represents the semantic meaning of a single placeholder.
    /// This includes:
    /// - Placeholder type (Title, Content, Subtitle, Chart, Table, Media, Body, Custom)
    /// - Whether the placeholder is required by the layout
    /// - Whether the placeholder is empty
    /// - Whether the placeholder was auto-generated by PowerPoint
    /// - Whether the user modified it
    /// - Whether the placeholder contains meaningful content
    ///
    /// No geometry is stored here — geometry is handled by PlaceholderGeometryData.
    /// </summary>
    public class PlaceholderMetadata
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Slide index for diagnostics and mapping.
        /// </summary>
        public int SlideIndex { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// PowerPoint's internal shape ID for the placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public int ShapeId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The semantic placeholder type (Title, Subtitle, Body, Chart, Table, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderTypeENUM PlaceholderType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if this placeholder is required by the slide layout.
        /// Example: Title placeholders are usually required.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsRequired { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder contains no meaningful content.
        /// Used by IssueDetector for "empty placeholder" warnings.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsEmpty { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if PowerPoint auto-generated this placeholder.
        /// Example: Some layouts auto-create a subtitle placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsAutoGenerated { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the user modified the placeholder (text, formatting, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsUserModified { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder contains meaningful content.
        /// This is different from IsEmpty because some placeholders contain
        /// whitespace or formatting but no real content.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsActive { get; set; }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Metadata
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Raw placeholder metadata extracted directly from PowerPoint.
    /// This is the extraction-layer counterpart to PlaceholderMetadataData (DTO).
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - Builders (PlaceholderMetadataBuilder, IssueDetector, OGMBuilder)
    ///       require semantic placeholder information.
    ///     - ShapeModel expects placeholder metadata to be attached at the shape level.
    ///     - DTO mapping requires stable, normalized metadata for server payloads.
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS REPRESENTS:
    ///     - Placeholder type (Title, Body, Picture, Chart, Table, etc.)
    ///     - Required/optional state
    ///     - Empty/active state
    ///     - Auto-generated vs user-modified
    ///     - Slide index + ShapeId for mapping
    ///
    /// This model is intentionally minimal and stable.
    /// </summary>
    public class PlaceholderMetadataExtraction
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Zero-based slide index where this placeholder lives.
        /// </summary>
        public int SlideIndex { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// PowerPoint's internal shape ID for the placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public int ShapeId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Semantic placeholder classification.
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderTypeENUM PlaceholderType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder is required by the slide layout.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsRequired { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder contains no meaningful content.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsEmpty { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if PowerPoint auto-generated this placeholder.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsAutoGenerated { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the user modified the placeholder (text, formatting, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsUserModified { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// True if the placeholder contains meaningful content.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsActive { get; set; }
    }
}

//
// RawChart.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects
//
// Represents raw chart data extracted from PowerPoint before mapping.
// Includes series, categories, axes, labels, and chart type.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools extraction layer.
//

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents raw chart data extracted from PowerPoint.
    /// This is the pre-mapped form used by ChartMapper.
    /// </summary>
    public class RawChart
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The chart type (Column, Bar, Line, Pie, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public string ChartType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The chart title text (if present).
        /// </summary>
        public string Title { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The category labels (X-axis labels).
        /// </summary>
        public List<string> Categories { get; set; } = new List<string>();

        /// <summary>
        /// The series contained in the chart.
        /// </summary>
        public List<RawChartSeries> Series { get; set; } = new List<RawChartSeries>();

        /// <summary>
        /// The axis titles (X and Y).
        /// </summary>
        public string AxisTitleX { get; set; }
        public string AxisTitleY { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Whether the chart has a legend.
        /// </summary>
        public bool HasLegend { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The legend position (Right, Left, Top, Bottom).
        /// </summary>
        public string LegendPosition { get; set; }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Represents a single chart series extracted from PowerPoint.
    /// </summary>
    public class RawChartSeries
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The series name.
        /// </summary>
        public string Name { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The numeric values in the series.
        /// </summary>
        public List<double> Values { get; set; } = new List<double>();

        /// <summary>
        /// The fill color for the series (if present).
        /// </summary>
        public string Fill { get; set; }
    }
}

//
// RawMedia.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects
//
// Represents raw media data extracted from PowerPoint before mapping.
// Includes video/audio metadata, playback settings, and source information.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools extraction layer.
//

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents raw media data extracted from PowerPoint.
    /// This is the pre-mapped form used by MediaMapper.
    /// </summary>
    public class RawMedia
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The media type (Video, Audio, GIF, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public string MediaType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The source path or URI of the media.
        /// </summary>
        public string Source { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Duration of the media in seconds (if applicable).
        /// </summary>
        public double Duration { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Whether the media is embedded inside the PPTX file.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsEmbedded { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Whether the media is linked externally.
        /// </summary>
        public bool IsLinked { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Whether the media auto-plays when the slide is shown.
        /// </summary>
        public bool AutoPlay { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Whether the media loops continuously.
        /// </summary>
        public bool Loop { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Playback volume (0–100).
        /// </summary>
        public int Volume { get; set; }
    }
}

//
// RawObject.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects
//
// Represents a raw extracted object from PowerPoint before mapping.
// Contains geometry, metadata, and type information used by ObjectMapper.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools extraction layer.
//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Geometry;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents a raw extracted object from PowerPoint.
    /// This is the pre-mapped form used by ObjectMapper to dispatch
    /// into ShapeDTO, ChartDTO, TableDTO, SmartArtDTO, PlaceholderDTO, MediaDTO.
    /// </summary>
    public class RawObject
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Unique ID assigned by PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public int ObjectId { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The raw object type (Shape, Chart, Table, SmartArt, Placeholder, Media).
        /// </summary>
        public RawObjectType ObjectType { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Name of the object as reported by PowerPoint.
        /// </summary>
        public string Name { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Z-order position on the slide.
        /// </summary>
        public int ZOrder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Slide index where this object resides.
        /// </summary>
        public int SlideIndex { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Geometry extracted from PowerPoint (bounding box, rotation, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public GeometryDTO Geometry { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw shape data (text, fill, border, etc.).
        /// </summary>
        public RawShape Shape { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw chart data (series, axes, labels).
        /// </summary>
        public RawChart Chart { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw table data (rows, columns, cell contents).
        /// </summary>
        public RawTable Table { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw SmartArt data (nodes, hierarchy).
        /// </summary>
        public RawSmartArt SmartArt { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw placeholder data (type, index, geometry).
        /// </summary>
        public RawPlaceholder Placeholder { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw media data (video/audio metadata).
        /// </summary>
        public RawMedia Media { get; set; }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;

{

    /// Represents raw slide data extracted from PowerPoint.
    /// This is the pre-mapped form used by SlideMapper.
    /// </summary>

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Represents raw SmartArt data extracted from PowerPoint.
    /// This is the pre-mapped form used by SmartArtMapper.
    /// </summary>

    {

        /// The SmartArt layout type (e.g., Hierarchy, Process, Cycle).

        /// <summary>

        /// </summary>

    }

        /// </summary>

        /// Whether this node is collapsed or expanded.
        /// </summary>

    }

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Interfaces
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Base contract for all builders in the Engine.
    /// A builder takes input context and produces a DTO.
    /// </summary>
    /// <typeparam name="TInput">Type of input context.</typeparam>
    /// <typeparam name="TOutput">Type of output DTO.</typeparam>
    public interface IBuilder<in TInput, out TOutput>
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Executes the builder logic and returns the output DTO.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="input">Input context.</param>
        /// <returns>Output DTO.</returns>
        TOutput Build(TInput input);
    }
}

//
// ChartMapper.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
//
// Maps RawObject chart structures into strongly typed ChartDTO objects.
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools mapping layer.
//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Chart;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Converts RawObject instances containing chart data into ChartDTO objects.
    /// Handles geometry, chart metadata, series, categories, data points,
    /// axis definitions, legends, titles, and formatting.
    /// </summary>
    public class ChartMapper
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RawObject into a ChartDTO.
        /// Returns a fully populated DTO with geometry and chart-specific data.
        /// </summary>
        public ChartDTO Map(RawObject raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return null;

            var dto = new ChartDTO
            {
                ObjectId = raw.ObjectId,
                ObjectType = raw.ObjectType,
                Name = raw.Name,
                ZOrder = raw.ZOrder,

                // Geometry
                X = raw.Geometry?.X ?? 0,
                Y = raw.Geometry?.Y ?? 0,
                Width = raw.Geometry?.Width ?? 0,
                Height = raw.Geometry?.Height ?? 0,
                Rotation = raw.Geometry?.Rotation ?? 0,

                // Chart metadata
                ChartType = raw.Chart?.ChartType,
                Series = raw.Chart?.Series,
                Categories = raw.Chart?.Categories,
                DataPoints = raw.Chart?.DataPoints,
                Axis = raw.Chart?.Axis,
                Legend = raw.Chart?.Legend,
                Title = raw.Chart?.Title,
                Formatting = raw.Chart?.Formatting
            };

            return dto;
        }
    }
}


FILE: DeckMapper.cs

FILE: Core\Mapping\DeckMapper.cs
FOLDER: Core\Mapping

LAST MODIFIED: 2026-07-24 13:06:49
SIZE: 3,666 bytes

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.MetaData;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Slide;
using System.Collections.Generic;

/// <summary>
/// ====================================================================================================
/// RAW ? DTO MAPPER : DECK (TOP-LEVEL ORCHESTRATOR)
/// ====================================================================================================
/// Converts RAW deck extraction results into a normalized DeckDTO used by SMARTtools engines.
///
/// ARCHITECTURE NOTES:
///     - DeckMapper is the TOP orchestrator above SlideMapper.
///     - It consumes SlideMapper for slide-level mapping.
///     - It consumes MetadataMapper and ThemeMapper for deck-level metadata.
///     - It produces DeckDTO, the root of the SMARTtools data model.
///
/// PURPOSE:
///     - Transform RAW deck extraction results into a strongly typed DeckDTO.
///     - Normalize deck-level metadata (authoring, timestamps, tags).
///     - Normalize deck-level theme (global theme + variants).
///     - Normalize deck-level notes.
///     - Normalize deck-level identity.
///     - Normalize slides via SlideMapper.
///     - Provide a clean, predictable structure for downstream engines:
///         * OGM (Object Geometry Model)
///         * IssueDetector
///         * PayloadBuilder
///         * DeckAuditEngine (future)
///         * SlideDiffEngine (future)
///
/// WHY THIS ARCHITECTURE:
///     - Keeps deck-specific logic isolated and maintainable.
///     - Allows future developers to modify deck mapping without touching slide-level mappers.
///     - Supports unit testing of deck mapping independently.
///     - Aligns with Microsoft-style modular mapping patterns.
///
/// FUTURE DEVELOPER NOTES:
///     - RAW deck models may evolve — update this mapper accordingly.
///     - Do NOT add slide routing logic here — that belongs in SlideMapper.
///     - Keep this class stateless and deterministic.
///     - Heavy comments are intentional — SMARTtools coding culture prioritizes clarity.
///
/// ====================================================================================================
/// </summary>

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    public class DeckMapper
    {
        private readonly SlideMapper _slideMapper = new SlideMapper();
        private readonly MetadataMapper _metadataMapper = new MetadataMapper();
        private readonly ThemeMapper _themeMapper = new ThemeMapper();
        private readonly IdentityMapper _identityMapper = new IdentityMapper();

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RAW deck into a normalized DeckDTO.
        /// </summary>
        public DeckDTO Map(RawDeck raw)
        {
            var dto = new DeckDTO
            {
                // Deck identity.
                DeckId = raw.DeckId,
                Name = raw.Name,

                // Deck metadata.
                Metadata = _metadataMapper.Map(raw.Metadata),

                // Deck theme.
                Theme = _themeMapper.Map(raw.Theme),

                // Deck notes.
                Notes = raw.Notes,

                // Deck-level identity metadata.
                Identity = _identityMapper.Map(raw.Identity),

                // Slides.
                Slides = new List<SlideDTO>()
            };

            // Map slides.
            if (raw.Slides != null)
            {
                foreach (var rawSlide in raw.Slides)
                {
                    dto.Slides.Add(_slideMapper.Map(rawSlide));
                }
            }

            return dto;
        }
    }
}


FILE: ExtractionToDTOMapper.cs
============================================================
FILE: Core\Mapping\ExtractionToDTOMapper.cs
FOLDER: Core\Mapping
FULL PATH: C:\Users\17068\source\repos\SMARTtools\Core\Mapping\ExtractionToDTOMapper.cs
LAST MODIFIED: 2026-07-24 13:06:49
SIZE: 2,902 bytes
============================================================

/// <summary>
/// ====================================================================================================
/// RAW ? DTO MAPPER : EXTRACTION ORCHESTRATOR (TOP OF THE PYRAMID)
/// ====================================================================================================
/// This is the FIRST mapper invoked after RAW extraction completes.
/// It converts the entire RAW extraction result (deck, slides, objects, metadata)
/// into a fully normalized DeckDTO hierarchy used by all SMARTtools engines.
///
/// ARCHITECTURE NOTES:
///     - This is the top-level orchestrator for the entire mapping layer.
///     - It consumes DeckMapper, which consumes SlideMapper, which consumes ObjectMapper.
///     - It is the SINGLE entry point for converting RAW ? DTO.
///     - All downstream engines depend on this class.
///
/// PURPOSE:
///     - Accept RAW extraction results from EngineContext.
///     - Normalize deck-level metadata, theme, identity, notes.
///     - Normalize slide-level metadata, theme, transitions, notes.
///     - Normalize object-level geometry, formatting, identity, content.
///     - Produce a complete DeckDTO ready for analysis, diagnostics, and payload generation.
///
/// WHY THIS ARCHITECTURE:
///     - Keeps mapping logic centralized and predictable.
///     - Allows future developers to modify mapping without touching engines.
///     - Supports unit testing of the entire mapping pipeline.
///     - Aligns with Microsoft-style multi-layer mapping patterns.
///
/// FUTURE DEVELOPER NOTES:
///     - RAW extraction models may evolve — update DeckMapper, SlideMapper, ObjectMapper accordingly.
///     - Do NOT add object routing logic here — that belongs in ObjectMapper.
///     - Keep this class stateless and deterministic.
///     - Heavy comments are intentional — SMARTtools coding culture prioritizes clarity.
///
/// ====================================================================================================
/// </summary>

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    public class ExtractionToDTOMapper
    {
        private readonly DeckMapper _deckMapper = new DeckMapper();

        /// <summary>
        /// Converts RAW extraction results into a fully normalized DeckDTO.
        /// </summary>
        public DeckDTO Map(RawExtractionResult raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return new DeckDTO(); // Graceful fallback.

            // DeckMapper handles everything below this level.
            var deckDto = _deckMapper.Map(raw.Deck);

            // Attach extraction-level metadata (if any).
            deckDto.ExtractionTimestamp = raw.ExtractionTimestamp;
            deckDto.SourceFilePath = raw.SourceFilePath;
            deckDto.EngineVersion = raw.EngineVersion;

            return deckDto;
        }
    }
}


FILE: GroupMapper.cs
============================================================

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;
using System.Collections.Generic;

/// <summary>
/// ====================================================================================================
/// RAW ? DTO MAPPER : GROUP OBJECTS
/// ====================================================================================================
/// Converts RAW Group objects into normalized GroupDTO instances used by SMARTtools engines.
///
/// ARCHITECTURE NOTES:
///     - This mapper handles ONLY group objects (ObjectTypeENUM.Group).
///     - Groups contain nested objects: shapes, charts, tables, SmartArt, media, placeholders, etc.
///     - ObjectMapper routes RAW objects to this mapper based on ObjectTypeENUM.
///     - SlideMapper consumes GroupDTO as part of SlideDTO.Objects.
///     - GroupMapper delegates nested object mapping BACK to ObjectMapper (recursive mapping).
///
/// PURPOSE:
///     - Transform RAW group extraction results into a strongly typed GroupDTO.
///     - Normalize geometry (X, Y, Width, Height, Rotation).
///     - Normalize group metadata (name, z-order, visibility).
///     - Normalize nested objects using ObjectMapper (recursive descent).
///     - Provide a clean, predictable structure for downstream engines:
///         * OGM (Object Geometry Model)
///         * IssueDetector
///         * PayloadBuilder
///
/// WHY THIS ARCHITECTURE:
///     - Keeps group-specific logic isolated and maintainable.
///     - Allows future developers to modify group mapping without touching other object types.
///     - Supports unit testing of group mapping independently.
///     - Aligns with Microsoft-style modular mapping patterns.
///     - Recursive mapping ensures nested objects behave identically to top-level objects.
///
/// FUTURE DEVELOPER NOTES:
///     - RAW group models may evolve — update this mapper accordingly.
///     - Do NOT add routing logic here — that belongs in ObjectMapper.
///     - Keep this class stateless and deterministic.
///     - Heavy comments are intentional — SMARTtools coding culture prioritizes clarity.
///
/// ====================================================================================================
/// </summary>

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    public class GroupMapper
    {
        private readonly ObjectMapper _objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RAW group object into a normalized GroupDTO.
        /// </summary>
        public GroupDTO Map(RawObject raw)
        {
            // Create the DTO container.
            var dto = new GroupDTO
            {
                ObjectId = raw.ObjectId,
                ObjectType = raw.ObjectType,
                Name = raw.Name,
                ZOrder = raw.ZOrder,

                // Geometry normalization.
                X = raw.Geometry?.X ?? 0,
                Y = raw.Geometry?.Y ?? 0,
                Width = raw.Geometry?.Width ?? 0,
                Height = raw.Geometry?.Height ?? 0,
                Rotation = raw.Geometry?.Rotation ?? 0,

                // Group metadata.
                IsCollapsed = raw.Group?.IsCollapsed ?? false,
                IsLocked = raw.Group?.IsLocked ?? false,

                // Nested objects.
                Children = new List<ObjectDTO>()
            };

            // Map nested objects using ObjectMapper (recursive).
            if (raw.Group?.Children != null)
            {
                var childDtos = _objectMapper.MapObjects(raw.Group.Children);
                dto.Children.AddRange(childDtos);
            }

            return dto;
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object;

/// <summary>
/// ====================================================================================================
/// RAW ? DTO MAPPER : IDENTITY METADATA
/// ====================================================================================================
/// Converts RAW identity metadata into normalized IdentityDTO instances used by SMARTtools engines.
///
/// ARCHITECTURE NOTES:
///     - This mapper handles ONLY identity metadata (ObjectTypeENUM applies at the object level).
///     - Identity metadata is attached to ALL object types: shapes, charts, tables, SmartArt, media, etc.
///     - SlideMapper and ObjectMapper rely on this mapper to ensure consistent identity normalization.
///     - IdentityMapper does NOT map geometry, formatting, or content — only identity.
///
/// PURPOSE:
///     - Transform RAW identity extraction results into a strongly typed IdentityDTO.
///     - Normalize stable identifiers (ObjectId, PersistentId, RevisionId).
///     - Normalize semantic identity (Role, Category, LogicalGrouping).
///     - Provide a clean, predictable structure for downstream engines:
///         * OGM (Object Geometry Model)
///         * IssueDetector
///         * PayloadBuilder
///         * SlideDiffEngine (future module)
///
/// WHY THIS ARCHITECTURE:
///     - Keeps identity-specific logic isolated and maintainable.
///     - Allows future developers to modify identity mapping without touching object-type mappers.
///     - Supports unit testing of identity mapping independently.
///     - Aligns with Microsoft-style modular mapping patterns.
///
/// FUTURE DEVELOPER NOTES:
///     - RAW identity models may evolve — update this mapper accordingly.
///     - Do NOT add routing logic here — that belongs in ObjectMapper.
///     - Keep this class stateless and deterministic.
///     - Heavy comments are intentional — SMARTtools coding culture prioritizes clarity.
///
/// ====================================================================================================
/// </summary>

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    public class IdentityMapper
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps RAW identity metadata into a normalized IdentityDTO.
        /// </summary>
        public IdentityDTO Map(RawIdentity raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return new IdentityDTO(); // Graceful fallback.

            var dto = new IdentityDTO
            {
                // Stable identifiers.
                ObjectId = raw.ObjectId,
                PersistentId = raw.PersistentId,
                RevisionId = raw.RevisionId,

                // Semantic identity.
                Role = raw.Role,
                Category = raw.Category,
                LogicalGroup = raw.LogicalGroup,

                // Flags.
                IsGenerated = raw.IsGenerated,
                IsUserCreated = raw.IsUserCreated,
                IsTemplateObject = raw.IsTemplateObject
            };

            return dto;
        }
    }
}

//
// MediaMapper.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
//
// Maps RawMedia objects into MediaDTO records.
// Handles video, audio, and other embedded media geometry + metadata.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools mapping layer.
//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Media;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Maps RawMedia instances into strongly typed MediaDTO objects.
    /// </summary>
    public class MediaMapper
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RawObject (media) into a MediaDTO.
        /// Returns null if the raw object is not media.
        /// </summary>
        public MediaDTO Map(RawObject raw)
        {
            if (raw == null || raw.Media == null)
                return null;

            var media = raw.Media;

            return new MediaDTO
            {
                ObjectId = raw.ObjectId,
                ObjectType = raw.ObjectType,
                Name = raw.Name,
                ZOrder = raw.ZOrder,

                // Geometry
                Geometry = raw.Geometry,

                // Media-specific fields
                MediaType = media.MediaType,
                Source = media.Source,
                Duration = media.Duration,
                IsEmbedded = media.IsEmbedded,
                IsLinked = media.IsLinked,

                // Playback properties
                AutoPlay = media.AutoPlay,
                Loop = media.Loop,
                Volume = media.Volume
            };
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Presentation;

/// <summary>
/// ====================================================================================================
/// RAW ? DTO MAPPER : METADATA
/// ====================================================================================================
/// Converts RAW metadata into normalized MetadataDTO instances used by SMARTtools engines.
///
/// ARCHITECTURE NOTES:
///     - This mapper handles ONLY metadata (not geometry, identity, or formatting).
///     - Metadata applies to objects, slides, and decks.
///     - SlideMapper and DeckMapper rely on this mapper to normalize descriptive metadata.
///     - MetadataMapper does NOT map identity, geometry, or content — only descriptive attributes.
///
/// PURPOSE:
///     - Transform RAW metadata extraction results into a strongly typed MetadataDTO.
///     - Normalize authoring metadata (CreatedBy, ModifiedBy).
///     - Normalize timestamps (CreatedOn, ModifiedOn).
///     - Normalize descriptive metadata (Tags, Comments, Notes, CustomProperties).
///     - Provide a clean, predictable structure for downstream engines:
///         * IssueDetector
///         * PayloadBuilder
///         * DeckAuditEngine (future module)
///         * SlideDiffEngine (future module)
///
/// WHY THIS ARCHITECTURE:
///     - Keeps metadata-specific logic isolated and maintainable.
///     - Allows future developers to modify metadata mapping without touching object-type mappers.
///     - Supports unit testing of metadata mapping independently.
///     - Aligns with Microsoft-style modular mapping patterns.
///
/// FUTURE DEVELOPER NOTES:
///     - RAW metadata models may evolve — update this mapper accordingly.
///     - Do NOT add routing logic here — that belongs in ObjectMapper or SlideMapper.
///     - Keep this class stateless and deterministic.
///     - Heavy comments are intentional — SMARTtools coding culture prioritizes clarity.
///
/// ====================================================================================================
/// </summary>

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    public class MetadataMapper
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps RAW metadata into a normalized MetadataDTO.
        /// </summary>
        public MetadataDTO Map(RawMetadata raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return new MetadataDTO(); // Graceful fallback.

            var dto = new MetadataDTO
            {
                // Authoring metadata.
                CreatedBy = raw.CreatedBy,
                ModifiedBy = raw.ModifiedBy,

                // Timestamps.
                CreatedOn = raw.CreatedOn,
                ModifiedOn = raw.ModifiedOn,

                // Descriptive metadata.
                Tags = raw.Tags,
                Comments = raw.Comments,
                Notes = raw.Notes,

                // Custom properties.
                CustomProperties = raw.CustomProperties
            };

            return dto;
        }
    }
}

//
// ObjectMapper.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
//
// Dispatches RawObject instances to the correct specialized mapper
// (ShapeMapper, ChartMapper, TableMapper, SmartArtMapper, PlaceholderMapper, MediaMapper).
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools mapping layer.
//

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Central dispatcher that maps RawObject instances into strongly typed ObjectDTOs.
    /// Delegates to specialized mappers based on object type.
    /// </summary>
    public class ObjectMapper
    {
        private readonly ShapeMapper _shapeMapper = new ShapeMapper();
        private readonly ChartMapper _chartMapper = new ChartMapper();
        private readonly TableMapper _tableMapper = new TableMapper();
        private readonly SmartArtMapper _smartArtMapper = new SmartArtMapper();
        private readonly PlaceholderMapper _placeholderMapper = new PlaceholderMapper();
        private readonly MediaMapper _mediaMapper = new MediaMapper();

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a collection of RawObject instances into ObjectDTOs.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ObjectDTO> MapObjects(IEnumerable<RawObject> rawObjects)
        {
            var results = new List<ObjectDTO>();

            if (rawObjects == null)
                return results;

            foreach (var raw in rawObjects)
            {
                var dto = Map(raw);
                if (dto != null)
                    results.Add(dto);
            }

            return results;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a single RawObject into the correct ObjectDTO subtype.
        /// </summary>
        public ObjectDTO Map(RawObject raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return null;

            return raw.ObjectType switch
            {
                RawObjectType.Shape => _shapeMapper.Map(raw),
                RawObjectType.Chart => _chartMapper.Map(raw),
                RawObjectType.Table => _tableMapper.Map(raw),
                RawObjectType.SmartArt => _smartArtMapper.Map(raw),
                RawObjectType.Placeholder => _placeholderMapper.Map(raw),
                RawObjectType.Media => _mediaMapper.Map(raw),

                _ => new ObjectDTO
                {
                    ObjectId = raw.ObjectId,
                    ObjectType = raw.ObjectType,
                    Name = raw.Name,
                    ZOrder = raw.ZOrder,
                    Geometry = raw.Geometry
                }
            };
        }
    }
}

//
// PlaceholderMapper.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
//
// Maps RawObject placeholder structures into strongly typed PlaceholderDTO objects.
// Handles geometry, formatting, placeholder metadata, and delegates text mapping
// to the TextMapper.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools mapping layer.
//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Placeholder;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Converts RawObject instances containing placeholder data into PlaceholderDTO objects.
    /// Handles geometry, placeholder metadata, formatting, and optional text content.
    /// </summary>
    public class PlaceholderMapper
    {
        private readonly TextMapper _textMapper = new TextMapper();

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RawObject into a PlaceholderDTO.
        /// Returns a fully populated DTO with geometry, formatting,
        /// placeholder metadata, and optional text content.
        /// </summary>
        public PlaceholderDTO Map(RawObject raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return null;

            var dto = new PlaceholderDTO
            {
                ObjectId = raw.ObjectId,
                ObjectType = raw.ObjectType,
                Name = raw.Name,
                ZOrder = raw.ZOrder,

                // Geometry
                X = raw.Geometry?.X ?? 0,
                Y = raw.Geometry?.Y ?? 0,
                Width = raw.Geometry?.Width ?? 0,
                Height = raw.Geometry?.Height ?? 0,
                Rotation = raw.Geometry?.Rotation ?? 0,

                // Placeholder metadata
                PlaceholderType = raw.Placeholder?.PlaceholderType,
                PlaceholderIndex = raw.Placeholder?.PlaceholderIndex,
                SemanticRole = raw.Placeholder?.SemanticRole,

                // Formatting
                Fill = raw.Placeholder?.Fill,
                Line = raw.Placeholder?.Line,
                Alignment = raw.Placeholder?.Alignment,

                // Text populated below if present
                Text = null
            };

            // Map placeholder text if available
            if (raw.Placeholder?.Text != null)
            {
                dto.Text = _textMapper.MapPlaceholderText(raw.Placeholder.Text);
            }

            return dto;
        }
    }
}

//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Shape;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{

    /// Converts RawObject instances into ShapeDTO objects.
    /// Handles geometry, fill, line formatting, and delegates text mapping

    {
        private readonly TextMapper _textMapper = new TextMapper();

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RawObject into a ShapeDTO.
        /// Returns a fully populated DTO with geometry, formatting,
        /// and optional text content.
        /// </summary>
        public ShapeDTO Map(RawObject raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return null;

            var dto = new ShapeDTO
            {
                ObjectId = raw.ObjectId,
                ObjectType = raw.ObjectType,
                Name = raw.Name,
                ZOrder = raw.ZOrder,

                // Geometry
                X = raw.Geometry?.X ?? 0,
                Y = raw.Geometry?.Y ?? 0,
                Width = raw.Geometry?.Width ?? 0,
                Height = raw.Geometry?.Height ?? 0,
                Rotation = raw.Geometry?.Rotation ?? 0,

                // Formatting
                Fill = raw.Fill,
                Line = raw.Line,

                // Text populated below if present
                Text = null
            };

            // Map text content if available
            if (raw.Text != null)
            {
                dto.Text = _textMapper.Map(raw);
            }

            return dto;
        }
    }

//
// SlideMapper.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
//
// Maps RawSlide structures into strongly typed SlideDTO objects.
// Handles slide metadata, theme, transition, background, notes,
// and delegates object mapping to ObjectMapper.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools mapping layer.
//

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Object;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Slide;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Converts RawSlide instances into SlideDTO objects.
    /// Handles slide-level metadata, theme, transitions, background,
    /// notes, and object collections.
    /// </summary>
    public class SlideMapper
    {
        private readonly ObjectMapper _objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();
        private readonly MetadataMapper _metadataMapper = new MetadataMapper();
        private readonly ThemeMapper _themeMapper = new ThemeMapper();
        private readonly TransitionMapper _transitionMapper = new TransitionMapper();
        private readonly IdentityMapper _identityMapper = new IdentityMapper();

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RawSlide into a SlideDTO.
        /// Returns a fully populated DTO with metadata, theme,
        /// transition, background, notes, and object collection.
        /// </summary>
        public SlideDTO Map(RawSlide raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return null;

            var dto = new SlideDTO
            {
                SlideId = raw.SlideId,
                Index = raw.Index,
                LayoutName = raw.LayoutName,
                Title = raw.Title,

                // Metadata
                Metadata = _metadataMapper.Map(raw.Metadata),

                // Theme
                Theme = _themeMapper.Map(raw.Theme),

                // Transition
                Transition = _transitionMapper.Map(raw.Transition),

                // Notes & Background
                Notes = raw.Notes,
                Background = raw.Background,

                // Objects populated below
                Objects = new List<ObjectDTO>()
            };

            // Map slide objects
            if (raw.Objects != null)
            {
                dto.Objects = _objectMapper.MapObjects(raw.Objects);
            }

            return dto;
        }
    }
}

//
// SmartArtMapper.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
//
// Maps RawSmartArt structures into strongly typed SmartArtDTO objects.
// Handles geometry, formatting, layout metadata, and hierarchical node mapping.
// Delegates node text mapping to TextMapper.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools mapping layer.
//

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.SmartArt;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Converts RawSmartArt instances into SmartArtDTO objects.
    /// Handles geometry, layout metadata, formatting, and recursive node mapping.
    /// </summary>
    public class SmartArtMapper
    {
        private readonly TextMapper _textMapper = new TextMapper();

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RawSmartArt object into a SmartArtDTO.
        /// Returns a fully populated DTO with geometry, formatting,
        /// layout metadata, and hierarchical node structure.
        /// </summary>
        public SmartArtDTO Map(RawSmartArt raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return null;

            var dto = new SmartArtDTO
            {
                ObjectId = raw.ObjectId,
                ObjectType = raw.ObjectType,
                Name = raw.Name,
                ZOrder = raw.ZOrder,

                // Geometry
                X = raw.Geometry?.X ?? 0,
                Y = raw.Geometry?.Y ?? 0,
                Width = raw.Geometry?.Width ?? 0,
                Height = raw.Geometry?.Height ?? 0,
                Rotation = raw.Geometry?.Rotation ?? 0,

                // SmartArt metadata
                LayoutType = raw.LayoutType,
                Style = raw.Style,
                ColorSet = raw.ColorSet,

                // Node hierarchy
                Nodes = new List<SmartArtNodeDTO>()
            };

            // Map SmartArt nodes recursively
            if (raw.Nodes != null)
            {
                foreach (var rawNode in raw.Nodes)
                {
                    dto.Nodes.Add(MapNode(rawNode));
                }
            }

            return dto;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Recursively maps a RawSmartArtNode into a SmartArtNodeDTO.
        /// Handles geometry, formatting, text, and child node hierarchy.
        /// </summary>
        private SmartArtNodeDTO MapNode(RawSmartArtNode rawNode)
        {
            if (rawNode == null)
                return null;

            var nodeDto = new SmartArtNodeDTO
            {
                NodeId = rawNode.NodeId,
                Level = rawNode.Level,
                Order = rawNode.Order,

                // Geometry
                X = rawNode.Geometry?.X ?? 0,
                Y = rawNode.Geometry?.Y ?? 0,
                Width = rawNode.Geometry?.Width ?? 0,
                Height = rawNode.Geometry?.Height ?? 0,

                // Formatting
                Fill = rawNode.Fill,
                Line = rawNode.Line,

                // Text content
                Text = rawNode.Text != null
                    ? _textMapper.MapNodeText(rawNode.Text)
                    : null,

                // Child nodes
                Children = new List<SmartArtNodeDTO>()
            };

            // Recursively map children
            if (rawNode.Children != null)
            {
                foreach (var child in rawNode.Children)
                {
                    nodeDto.Children.Add(MapNode(child));
                }
            }

            return nodeDto;
        }
    }
}

//
// TableMapper.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
//
// Maps RawObject table structures into strongly typed TableDTO objects.
// Handles rows, cells, spans, formatting, and delegates text mapping
// to the TextMapper.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools mapping layer.
//

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Table;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Converts RawObject instances containing table data into TableDTO objects.
    /// Handles geometry, formatting, row/column structure, and cell-level text.
    /// </summary>
    public class TableMapper
    {
        private readonly TextMapper _textMapper = new TextMapper();

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RawObject into a TableDTO.
        /// Returns a fully populated DTO with geometry, formatting,
        /// row/column definitions, and cell-level content.
        /// </summary>
        public TableDTO Map(RawObject raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return null;

            var dto = new TableDTO
            {
                ObjectId = raw.ObjectId,
                ObjectType = raw.ObjectType,
                Name = raw.Name,
                ZOrder = raw.ZOrder,

                // Geometry
                X = raw.Geometry?.X ?? 0,
                Y = raw.Geometry?.Y ?? 0,
                Width = raw.Geometry?.Width ?? 0,
                Height = raw.Geometry?.Height ?? 0,
                Rotation = raw.Geometry?.Rotation ?? 0,

                // Table structure
                Columns = raw.Table?.ColumnCount ?? 0,
                Rows = new List<TableRowDTO>(),

                // Formatting
                Formatting = raw.Table?.Formatting
            };

            // Map row and cell structures
            if (raw.Table?.Rows != null)
            {
                foreach (var rawRow in raw.Table.Rows)
                {
                    var rowDto = new TableRowDTO
                    {
                        RowIndex = rawRow.RowIndex,
                        Cells = new List<TableCellDTO>()
                    };

                    foreach (var rawCell in rawRow.Cells)
                    {
                        var cellDto = new TableCellDTO
                        {
                            RowIndex = rawCell.RowIndex,
                            ColumnIndex = rawCell.ColumnIndex,
                            RowSpan = rawCell.RowSpan,
                            ColumnSpan = rawCell.ColumnSpan,

                            // Formatting
                            Fill = rawCell.Fill,
                            Borders = rawCell.Borders,
                            Alignment = rawCell.Alignment,

                            // Text content
                            Text = rawCell.Text != null
                                ? _textMapper.MapCellText(rawCell.Text)
                                : null
                        };

                        rowDto.Cells.Add(cellDto);
                    }

                    dto.Rows.Add(rowDto);
                }
            }

            return dto;
        }
    }
}

//
// TextMapper.cs
// SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
//
// Maps RawObject and RawText structures into strongly typed TextDTO objects.
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools mapping layer.
//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Shape;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.RawObjects;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Provides mapping utilities for converting raw text extraction objects
    /// into normalized TextDTO instances used throughout SMARTtools.
    /// </summary>
    public class TextMapper
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a RawObject containing text into a TextDTO.
        /// Returns null if the raw object or its text payload is missing.
        /// </summary>
        public TextDTO Map(RawObject raw)
        {
            if (raw?.Text == null)
                return null;

            return new TextDTO
            {
                Content = raw.Text.Content,
                FontName = raw.Text.FontName,
                FontSize = raw.Text.FontSize,
                FontColor = raw.Text.FontColor,
                IsBold = raw.Text.IsBold,
                IsItalic = raw.Text.IsItalic
            };
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps placeholder text (RawText) into a TextDTO.
        /// Used for title/subtitle/body placeholders.
        /// </summary>
        public TextDTO MapPlaceholderText(RawText rawText)
        {
            if (rawText == null)
                return null;

            return new TextDTO
            {
                Content = rawText.Content,
                FontName = rawText.FontName,
                FontSize = rawText.FontSize,
                FontColor = rawText.FontColor,
                IsBold = rawText.IsBold,
                IsItalic = rawText.IsItalic
            };
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps table cell text into a TextDTO.
        /// </summary>
        public TextDTO MapCellText(RawText rawText)
        {
            if (rawText == null)
                return null;

            return new TextDTO
            {
                Content = rawText.Content,
                FontName = rawText.FontName,
                FontSize = rawText.FontSize,
                FontColor = rawText.FontColor,
                IsBold = rawText.IsBold,
                IsItalic = rawText.IsItalic
            };
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Maps SmartArt node text into a TextDTO.
        /// </summary>
        public TextDTO MapNodeText(RawText rawText)
        {
            if (rawText == null)
                return null;

            return new TextDTO
            {
                Content = rawText.Content,
                FontName = rawText.FontName,
                FontSize = rawText.FontSize,
                FontColor = rawText.FontColor,
                IsBold = rawText.IsBold,
                IsItalic = rawText.IsItalic
            };
        }
    }
}
