using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Slide;

/// <summary>
/// ====================================================================================================
/// RAW ? DTO MAPPER : THEME METADATA
/// ====================================================================================================
/// Converts RAW theme metadata into normalized ThemeDTO instances used by SMARTtools engines.
///
/// ARCHITECTURE NOTES:
///     - This mapper handles ONLY theme metadata (colors, fonts, backgrounds, variants).
///     - Themes apply at the deck level AND slide level (inheritance model).
///     - SlideMapper and DeckMapper rely on this mapper to normalize theme information.
///     - ThemeMapper does NOT map geometry, identity, or content — only theme attributes.
///
/// PURPOSE:
///     - Transform RAW theme extraction results into a strongly typed ThemeDTO.
///     - Normalize color sets (Accent1–Accent6, Dark/Light variants).
///     - Normalize font sets (HeadingFont, BodyFont).
///     - Normalize background styles (fills, images, patterns).
///     - Normalize theme variants (PowerPoint theme families).
///     - Provide a clean, predictable structure for downstream engines:
///         * IssueDetector
///         * PayloadBuilder
///         * DeckAuditEngine (future module)
///         * SlideDiffEngine (future module)
///
/// WHY THIS ARCHITECTURE:
///     - Keeps theme-specific logic isolated and maintainable.
///     - Allows future developers to modify theme mapping without touching object-type mappers.
///     - Supports unit testing of theme mapping independently.
///     - Aligns with Microsoft-style modular mapping patterns.
///
/// FUTURE DEVELOPER NOTES:
///     - RAW theme models may evolve — update this mapper accordingly.
///     - Do NOT add routing logic here — that belongs in SlideMapper or DeckMapper.
///     - Keep this class stateless and deterministic.
///     - Heavy comments are intentional — SMARTtools coding culture prioritizes clarity.
///
/// ====================================================================================================
/// </summary>

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Mapping
{
    public class ThemeMapper
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps RAW theme metadata into a normalized ThemeDTO.
        /// </summary>
        public ThemeDTO Map(RawTheme raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return new ThemeDTO(); // Graceful fallback.

            var dto = new ThemeDTO
            {
                // Color sets.
                Accent1 = raw.Colors?.Accent1,
                Accent2 = raw.Colors?.Accent2,
                Accent3 = raw.Colors?.Accent3,
                Accent4 = raw.Colors?.Accent4,
                Accent5 = raw.Colors?.Accent5,
                Accent6 = raw.Colors?.Accent6,

                Dark1 = raw.Colors?.Dark1,
                Dark2 = raw.Colors?.Dark2,
                Light1 = raw.Colors?.Light1,
                Light2 = raw.Colors?.Light2,

                // Font sets.
                HeadingFont = raw.Fonts?.HeadingFont,
                BodyFont = raw.Fonts?.BodyFont,

                // Background styles.
                BackgroundFill = raw.Background?.Fill,
                BackgroundImage = raw.Background?.Image,
                BackgroundPattern = raw.Background?.Pattern,

                // Theme variant.
                VariantName = raw.Variant?.Name,
                VariantIndex = raw.Variant?.Index
            };

            return dto;
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Pipeline;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// ENGINE CONTEXT (v3, CLEANED)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// EngineContext represents the PIPELINE STATE for a single EngineShell run.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - The old architecture mixed pipeline state with output state.
    ///     - EngineContext previously contained OGM, Issues, and even payloads.
    ///     - That made the engine brittle and caused dozens of errors.
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS VERSION CONTAINS:
    ///     ? Raw extraction (PowerPointContext)
    ///     ? Normalized text
    ///     ? Geometry models
    ///     ? Placeholder geometry + metadata
    ///     ? Media geometry
    ///     ? Animation geometry
    ///     ? ShapeModels (interpreted shapes)
    ///     ? SlideModels (interpreted slides)
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS VERSION DOES NOT CONTAIN:
    ///     ? OGM (belongs in EngineShellResult)
    ///     ? Issues (belongs in EngineShellResult)
    ///     ? ServerPayload (belongs in EngineShellResult)
    ///     ? DeckDTO (belongs in EngineShellResult)
    ///     ? Any DTO lists (DeckDTO handles all DTOs)
    ///
    /// MVP PHILOSOPHY:
    ///     - EngineContext = pipeline state ONLY.
    ///     - EngineShellResult = output state ONLY.
    ///     - Builders populate EngineContext.
    ///     - Mapper converts EngineContext ? DeckDTO.
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class EngineContext
    {
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // RAW EXTRACTION (from InteropWrapper)
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw PowerPoint extraction context.
        /// Contains slides, text shapes, media shapes, and raw geometry.
        /// </summary>
        public PowerPointContext PowerPointContext { get; set; }


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // NORMALIZED TEXT
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalized text objects produced by TextNormalizationBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public List<NormalizedText> NormalizedText { get; set; } = new List<NormalizedText>();


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // GEOMETRY MODELS
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Geometry models for shapes, produced by GeometryBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public List<GeometryData> Geometry { get; set; } = new List<GeometryData>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Placeholder geometry models, produced by PlaceholderGeometryBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public List<PlaceholderGeometryData> PlaceholderGeometry { get; set; } = new List<PlaceholderGeometryData>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Placeholder metadata models, produced by PlaceholderMetadataBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public List<PlaceholderMetadataData> PlaceholderMetadata { get; set; } = new List<PlaceholderMetadataData>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Animation geometry models, produced by AnimationGeometryBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public List<AnimationGeometryData> Animations { get; set; } = new List<AnimationGeometryData>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Media geometry models, produced by MediaGeometryBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public List<MediaGeometryData> MediaGeometry { get; set; } = new List<MediaGeometryData>();


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // INTERPRETED MODELS
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Fully interpreted shape models used by builders and OGMBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public List<ShapeModel> ShapeModels { get; set; } = new List<ShapeModel>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Fully interpreted slide models used by builders and OGMBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public List<SlideModel> SlideModels { get; set; } = new List<SlideModel>();


        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
        // CONSTRUCTOR
        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Default constructor.
        /// EngineShell creates and populates EngineContext.
        /// </summary>
        public EngineContext() { }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// EngineShellResult represents the FINAL OUTPUT of a single EngineShell run.
    /// Now aligned to the new architecture using DeckDTO as the authoritative DTO container.
    /// </summary>
    public class EngineShellResult
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// The complete DTO representation of the deck.
        /// Produced by ExtractionToDTOMapper.
        /// </summary>
        public DeckDTO Deck { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// The object graph model (OGM) produced by OGMBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public OGM OGM { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// All issues detected by IssueDetector.
        /// </summary>
        public List<IssueDTO> Issues { get; set; } = new List<IssueDTO>();

        /// <summary>
        /// The final server payload produced by ServerPayloadBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public ServerPayload Payload { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Version of the engine that produced this result.
        /// </summary>
        public string EngineVersion { get; set; } = "SMARTtools.Engine v3";

        /// <summary>
        /// Timestamp when the engine completed processing (ISO-8601 UTC).
        /// </summary>
        public string CompletedAtUtc { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o");

        /// <summary>
        /// Identifies the originating application.
        /// </summary>
        public string SourceApplication { get; set; } = "SMARTtools";
    }
}

using System;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Pipeline
{
    /// <summary>
    /// PayloadVersion represents the semantic version of the ServerPayload
    /// produced during a single SMARTtools engine run.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS EXISTS:
    ///     - Downstream modules (SMARTshare, SMARTcomply, SMARTcast, SMARTmanage)
    ///       must know whether a payload is fresh, stale, rebuilt, or invalidated.
    ///
    /// RULES:
    ///     ? Immutable once created
    ///     ? Monotonic version identifier
    ///     ? Timestamped for freshness comparison
    ///     ? Source-aware (full vs partial rebuild)
    ///
    /// This class is intentionally tiny and future-proof.
    /// </summary>
    public class PayloadVersion
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Monotonic version identifier for the payload.
        /// Incremented on every successful rebuild.
        /// </summary>
        public int VersionId { get; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Timestamp when this payload version was created.
        /// </summary>
        public DateTime Timestamp { get; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Indicates whether the payload was produced from a full rebuild
        /// or a partial rebuild.
        /// </summary>
        public PayloadSource Source { get; }

        public PayloadVersion(int versionId = 1, PayloadSource source = PayloadSource.FullRebuild)
        {
            VersionId = versionId;
            Source = source;
            Timestamp = DateTime.UtcNow;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Indicates whether the payload was generated from a full or partial rebuild.
    /// </summary>
    public enum PayloadSource
    {
        FullRebuild,
        PartialRebuild
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO
{
    /// <summary>
    /// BoundingBoxSDTO represents a normalized bounding rectangle
    /// for any geometric object, measured in EMUs.
    ///
    /// This is rotation-aware and flip-aware.
    /// </summary>
    public class BoundingBoxSDTO
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// X position of the top-left corner (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public long X_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Y position of the top-left corner (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public long Y_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Width of the bounding box (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public long Width_EMU { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Height of the bounding box (in EMUs).
        /// </summary>
        public long Height_EMU { get; set; }
    }

using System;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Utils
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Provides helper math functions for geometry calculations.
    /// Used by GeometryBuilder and OGMBuilder.
    /// </summary>
    public static class GeometryMath
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Computes the Euclidean distance between two points.
        /// </summary>
        public static double Distance(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2)
        {
            double dx = x2 - x1;
            double dy = y2 - y1;
            return Math.Sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Normalizes a rotation angle into the range 0–360 degrees.
        /// </summary>
        public static double NormalizeAngle(double angle)
        {
            angle %= 360.0;
            return angle < 0 ? angle + 360.0 : angle;
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Utils
{
    /// <summary>
    /// MediaUtils provides helper functions for classifying media types
    /// based on file extensions. This utility is used by:
    ///
    ///   • MediaGeometryBuilder  – to determine how to extract geometry
    ///   • IssueDetector         – to determine which diagnostic rules apply
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    ///   This project targets .NET Framework 4.8 and C# 7.3 due to VSTO
    ///   constraints. Therefore, modern C# features such as:
    ///
    ///       • switch expressions
    ///       • pattern matching
    ///       • "or" patterns
    ///
    ///   are NOT available. All logic must be written using classic
    ///   if/else constructs to maintain compatibility.
    ///
    /// This file intentionally avoids modern syntax to ensure long-term
    /// stability and compatibility with the COM-based Office automation stack.
    /// </summary>
    public static class MediaUtils
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a file extension (".jpg", ".mp4", etc.) to a MediaType enum.
        ///
        /// WHY THIS MATTERS:
        ///   SMARTtools needs to know what kind of media it is dealing with
        ///   BEFORE geometry extraction or diagnostics occur. For example:
        ///
        ///       • Images ? bounding box geometry, DPI checks
        ///       • Videos ? placeholder geometry, aspect ratio checks
        ///       • Audio  ? icon geometry, metadata checks
        ///       • SVG    ? vector geometry, scaling checks
        ///
        ///   This method provides the first step in that classification.
        ///
        /// BEHAVIOR:
        ///   - Input is normalized to lowercase.
        ///   - Unknown extensions default to EmbeddedObject.
        ///   - Null/empty input returns MediaType.None.
        ///
        /// NOTE:
        ///   This method intentionally uses simple boolean chains instead of
        ///   modern pattern matching to remain compatible with C# 7.3.
        /// </summary>
        public static MediaTypeENUM GetMediaTypeFromExtension(string extension)
        {
            // Defensive check: null, empty, or whitespace cannot be classified.
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(extension))
                return MediaTypeENUM.None;

            // Normalize extension for consistent comparison.
            extension = extension.ToLowerInvariant();

            // IMAGE FORMATS ----------------------------------------------------
            // These are standard raster formats commonly used in PowerPoint.
            if (extension == ".jpg" ||
                extension == ".jpeg" ||
                extension == ".png" ||
                extension == ".bmp" ||
                extension == ".gif")
            {
                return MediaTypeENUM.Image;
            }

            // VIDEO FORMATS ----------------------------------------------------
            // PowerPoint supports embedded and linked video formats.
            if (extension == ".mp4" ||
                extension == ".mov" ||
                extension == ".avi" ||
                extension == ".wmv")
            {
                return MediaTypeENUM.Video;
            }

            // AUDIO FORMATS ----------------------------------------------------
            // Audio files appear as icon shapes with metadata.
            if (extension == ".mp3" ||
                extension == ".wav" ||
                extension == ".aac")
            {
                return MediaTypeENUM.Audio;
            }

            // SVG FORMAT -------------------------------------------------------
            // SVGs are vector graphics and require special geometry handling.
            if (extension == ".svg")
            {
                return MediaTypeENUM.Svg;
            }

            // FALLBACK ---------------------------------------------------------
            // Any unknown extension is treated as an embedded object.
            // This includes OLE objects, proprietary formats, and anything
            // PowerPoint stores as a generic binary blob.
            return MediaTypeENUM.EmbeddedObject;
        }
    }
}

using System;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Utils
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Provides helper functions for text normalization.
    /// Used by TextNormalizationBatch and IssueDetector.
    /// </summary>
    public static class NormalizationUtils
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Collapses multiple spaces into a single space.
        /// </summary>
        public static string CollapseSpaces(string input)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input))
                return input;

            return System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Replace(input, @"\s+", " ");
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Trims leading and trailing whitespace safely.
        /// </summary>
        public static string SafeTrim(string input)
        {
            return input?.Trim();
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Utils
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ShapeUtils
    ///
    /// Centralized helper utilities for shape-type classification.
    /// Converts raw PowerPoint shape type names into the stable
    /// ShapeTypeENUM identity used throughout SMARTtools.
    ///
    /// This mapping is used by:
    /// - Extraction (ContextSlides, ContextShapes)
    /// - GeometryBuilder
    /// - PlaceholderMetadataBuilder
    /// - ShapeModel mapping
    /// - IssueDetector
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    /// PowerPoint exposes shape types inconsistently across COM and OOXML.
    /// This utility normalizes those inconsistencies into a stable enum.
    /// </summary>
    public static class ShapeUtils
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a raw PowerPoint shape type name to a ShapeTypeENUM value.
        /// Input is normalized to lowercase for consistency.
        ///
        /// NOTE:
        /// This mapping intentionally focuses on high-level semantic identity.
        /// GeometryBuilder and PlaceholderMetadataBuilder handle deeper logic.
        /// </summary>
        public static ShapeTypeENUM MapShapeType(string typeName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(typeName))
                return ShapeTypeENUM.Unknown;

            typeName = typeName.ToLowerInvariant();

            switch (typeName)
            {
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Text
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                case "textbox":
                case "text":
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.TextBox;

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Pictures / Images
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                case "picture":
                case "image":
                case "photo":
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.PicturePlaceholder;

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Groups
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                case "group":
                case "groupshape":
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.Group;

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Charts
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                case "chart":
                case "chartplaceholder":
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.ChartPlaceholder;

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Tables
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                case "table":
                case "tableplaceholder":
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.TablePlaceholder;

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // SmartArt
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                case "smartart":
                case "smartartplaceholder":
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.SmartArtPlaceholder;

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Media
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                case "video":
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.Video;

                case "audio":
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.Audio;

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Generic placeholder
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                case "placeholder":
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.Placeholder;

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Unknown / fallback
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                default:
                    return ShapeTypeENUM.Unknown;
            }
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Utils
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Provides helper functions for slide classification.
    /// Used by ContextSlides and IssueDetector.
    /// </summary>
    public static class SlideUtils
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Maps a PowerPoint layout name to a SlideLayoutType enum.
        /// </summary>
        public static SlideLayoutType MapLayoutType(string layoutName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(layoutName))
                return SlideLayoutType.Unknown;

            layoutName = layoutName.ToLowerInvariant();

            switch (layoutName)
            {
                case "title slide":
                    return SlideLayoutType.Title;

                case "title and content":
                    return SlideLayoutType.TitleAndContent;

                case "section header":
                    return SlideLayoutType.SectionHeader;

                case "two content":
                    return SlideLayoutType.TwoContent;

                case "comparison":
                    return SlideLayoutType.Comparison;

                case "title only":
                    return SlideLayoutType.TitleOnly;

                case "blank":
                    return SlideLayoutType.Blank;

                default:
                    return SlideLayoutType.Custom;
            }
        }

    }
}

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Utils
{
    /// <summary>
    /// General-purpose text helper utilities.
    /// Used by TextNormalizationBuilder and IssueDetector.
    /// </summary>
    public static class TextUtils
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Returns true if the string contains visible text.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool HasVisibleText(string input)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(input))
                return false;

            return input.Trim().Length > 0;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Removes non-printable characters from text.
        /// </summary>
        public static string RemoveNonPrintable(string input)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input))
                return input;

            return System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Replace(input, @"[^\u0020-\u007E]", "");
        }
    }
}

//
// AnimationGeometryBuilder.cs
// SMARTtools.Engine.Builders
//
// Projects animation geometry from the PowerPoint extraction context into
// engine-level AnimationGeometryData records.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools engine builder layer.
//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Geometry;
using SMARTtools.Engine;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine.Builders
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Copies animation geometry from the PowerPointContext into the engine's
    /// AnimationGeometry collection for downstream analysis and reporting.
    /// </summary>
    public static class AnimationGeometryBuilder
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Executes the animation geometry projection for the current engine context.
        /// Safely returns if the PowerPointContext or its Animations collection
        /// is not available.
        /// </summary>
        public static void Run(EngineContext context)
        {
            if (context?.PowerPointContext?.Animations == null)
                return;

            foreach (var anim in context.PowerPointContext.Animations)
            {
                context.AnimationGeometry.Add(new AnimationGeometryData
                {
                    SlideIndex = anim.SlideIndex,
                    ShapeId = anim.ShapeId,
                    AnimationType = anim.AnimationType,
                    Duration = anim.Duration,
                    Delay = anim.Delay,
                    Trigger = anim.Trigger,
                    X = anim.X,
                    Y = anim.Y,
                    Width = anim.Width,
                    Height = anim.Height
                });
            }
        }
    }
}

using Microsoft.Office.Core;
using Microsoft.Office.Interop.PowerPoint;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Builds ContextMedia from the PowerPointInteropWrapper:
    /// media metadata and basic geometry.
    /// </summary>
    public static class ContextMediaBuilder
    {
        public static ContextMedia Build(PowerPointInteropWrapper ppt)
        {
            var result = new ContextMedia();

            if (ppt == null || ppt.ActivePresentation == null)
                return result;

            foreach (Slide slide in ppt.GetSlides())
            {
                foreach (Microsoft.Office.Interop.PowerPoint.Shape shape in ppt.GetShapes(slide))
                {
                    if (shape.Type != Microsoft.Office.Core.MsoShapeType.msoMedia)
                        continue;

                    // MEDIA DATA (resolution only)
                    var media = new MediaData
                    {
                        SlideIndex = slide.SlideIndex,
                        ShapeId = shape.Id,
                        Resolution = 0 // MVP: no resolution extraction yet
                    };
                    result.Media.Add(media);

                    // MEDIA GEOMETRY DATA (X,Y,W,H only)
                    var geom = new MediaGeometryData
                    {
                        SlideIndex = slide.SlideIndex,
                        ShapeId = shape.Id,
                        X = shape.Left,
                        Y = shape.Top,
                        Width = shape.Width,
                        Height = shape.Height
                    };
                    result.Geometry.Add(geom);
                }

            }

            return result;
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using Microsoft.Office.Interop.PowerPoint;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Pipeline;
namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// CONTEXT SLIDES BUILDER (SlideDTO Extraction Layer)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// PURPOSE:
    ///     Extract ONLY slide-level metadata from PowerPointInteropWrapper and populate SlideDTO objects.
    ///
    /// WHY THIS MATTERS:
    ///     - SlideDTO represents *slide metadata only*.
    ///     - Shapes are decomposed into TextDTO, MediaDTO, GeometryDTO, ObjectDTO, etc.
    ///     - IssueDetector, SMARTshare, SMARTcast all expect SlideDTO to be lightweight.
    ///
    /// DESIGN RULE:
    ///     DO NOT extract shapes, geometry, or media here.
    ///     Those belong to specialized builders (ContextTextShapesBuilder, GeometryBuilder, etc.).
    ///
    /// PIPELINE:
    ///     PowerPointInteropWrapper ? PowerPointContext.RawSlides ? ContextSlidesBuilder ? EngineContext.Slides
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public static class ContextSlidesBuilder
    {
        public static List<SlideDTO> Build(PowerPointInteropWrapper ppt)
        {
            var slides = new List<SlideDTO>();

            // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // SAFETY CHECK:
            // If wrapper or presentation is null, return empty list.
            // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            if (ppt == null || ppt.ActivePresentation == null)
                return slides;

            // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // SLIDE LOOP — Extract ONLY slide metadata
            // --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            foreach (Slide slide in ppt.GetSlides())
            {
                var dto = new SlideDTO
                {
                    Index = slide.SlideIndex,
                    HasTitlePlaceholder = ppt.HasTitlePlaceholder(slide)
                };

                slides.Add(dto);
            }

            return slides;
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Pipeline;
namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ContextTextShapesBuilder
    ///
    /// Extracts TextData objects from ShapeData by filtering shapes that
    /// contain text. SlideData does NOT contain shapes.
    ///
    /// Pipeline:
    /// PowerPointInteropWrapper ? PowerPointContext ? ContextShapesBuilder ? EngineContext.Shapes
    ///                                                        ?
    ///                                          ContextTextShapesBuilder ? EngineContext.TextShapes
    /// </summary>
    public static class ContextTextShapesBuilder
    {
        public static List<TextData> BuildTextShapes(List<ShapeData> shapes, int slideIndex)
        {
            var textShapes = new List<TextData>();

            if (shapes == null)
                return textShapes;

            foreach (var shape in shapes)
            {
                // Only shapes with actual text content
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(shape.TextContent))
                    continue;

                var text = new TextData
                {
                    ShapeId = shape.ShapeId,
                    SlideIndex = slideIndex,
                    Text = shape.TextContent   // <-- Correct property name
                };

                textShapes.Add(text);
            }

            return textShapes;
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Mapping;
using SMARTtools.Engine.Builders;
using SMARTtools.Engine.Issues;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ENGINE SHELL (v4.1)
    /// Fully aligned with EngineContext v3 (pipeline-only state).
    /// </summary>
    public class EngineShell
    {
        public EngineShellResult Run(PowerPointContext powerPointContext)
        {
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 1. CREATE PIPELINE CONTEXT
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            var context = new EngineContext
            {
                PowerPointContext = powerPointContext
            };

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 2. EXTRACTION ? NORMALIZATION ? GEOMETRY
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            GeometryBuilder.Run(context);
            TextNormalizationBuilder.Run(context);

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 3. PLACEHOLDER GEOMETRY + METADATA
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            PlaceholderGeometryBuilder.Run(context);
            PlaceholderMetadataBuilder.Run(context);

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 4. SLIDE-LEVEL PLACEHOLDER SEMANTICS
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            SlidePlaceholderNormalizer.Run(context);

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 5. MEDIA + ANIMATIONS
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            MediaGeometryBuilder.Run(context);
            AnimationGeometryBuilder.Run(context);

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 6. ISSUE DETECTION (output-only)
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            var issues = IssueDetector.Run(context);

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 7. BUILD OBJECT GRAPH MODEL (output-only)
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            var ogm = OGMBuilder.Build(context);

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 8. MAP ? DTOs (DeckDTO)
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            var deck = ExtractionToDTOMapper.Map(context);

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 9. ASSEMBLE FINAL RESULT
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            var result = new EngineShellResult
            {
                Deck = deck,
                OGM = ogm,
                Issues = issues
            };

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 10. BUILD SERVER PAYLOAD
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            result.Payload = ServerPayloadBuilder.Build(result);

            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            // 11. RETURN FINAL OUTPUT
            // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
            return result;
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Utilities;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.Pipeline
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// ENGINE STATE (PIPELINE LIFECYCLE CONTROLLER)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Tracks the lifecycle state of the SMARTtools engine during a single run.
    ///
    /// STATES:
    ///     Idle ? Extracting ? Normalizing ? Mapping ? Building ? PayloadReady
    ///
    /// EngineShell drives transitions; EngineState enforces them.
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class EngineState
    {
        public EngineLifecycleState CurrentState { get; private set; } = EngineLifecycleState.Idle;

        public void MoveToExtracting()
        {
            CurrentState = EngineLifecycleState.Extracting;
            Logging.Info("EngineState: Transition ? Extracting");
        }

        public void MoveToNormalizing()
        {
            CurrentState = EngineLifecycleState.Normalizing;
            Logging.Info("EngineState: Transition ? Normalizing");
        }

        public void MoveToMapping()
        {
            CurrentState = EngineLifecycleState.Mapping;
            Logging.Info("EngineState: Transition ? Mapping");
        }

        public void MoveToBuilding()
        {
            CurrentState = EngineLifecycleState.Building;
            Logging.Info("EngineState: Transition ? Building");
        }

        public void MoveToPayloadReady()
        {
            CurrentState = EngineLifecycleState.PayloadReady;
            Logging.Info("EngineState: Transition ? PayloadReady");
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Lifecycle states for the SMARTtools engine.
    /// </summary>
    public enum EngineLifecycleState
    {
        Idle,
        Extracting,
        Normalizing,
        Mapping,
        Building,
        PayloadReady
    }
}

//
// GeometryBuilder.cs
// SMARTtools.Engine
//
// Extracts geometric information from PowerPoint shapes and produces
// strongly typed ShapeData records used throughout the SMARTtools engine.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools engine layer.
//

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Microsoft.Office.Core;
using Microsoft.Office.Interop.PowerPoint;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction;
using SMARTtools.Core.StructuralDTO;
using SMARTtools.Utilities;
using PPTShape = Microsoft.Office.Interop.PowerPoint.Shape;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Builds geometric data for all shapes across all slides in a PowerPoint presentation.
    /// Produces ShapeData objects containing bounding boxes, rotation, fill/border flags,
    /// placeholder metadata, and other geometric attributes.
    /// </summary>
    public static class GeometryBuilder
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Extracts geometry for every shape in the presentation.
        /// Returns a list of ShapeData records.
        /// </summary>
        public static List<ShapeData> Build(Presentation presentation)
        {
            var shapes = new List<ShapeData>();

            if (presentation == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(presentation),
                    "Presentation cannot be null when building geometry.");
            }

            Logging.Info("[GeometryBuilder] Starting geometry extraction...");

            try
            {
                for (int slideIndex = 1; slideIndex <= presentation.Slides.Count; slideIndex++)
                {
                    var slide = presentation.Slides[slideIndex];

                    foreach (PPTShape shape in slide.Shapes)
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            var shapeData = BuildShapeGeometry(shape, slideIndex);
                            if (shapeData != null)
                            {
                                shapes.Add(shapeData);
                            }
                        }
                        catch (Exception ex)
                        {
                            Logging.Warn(
                                $"[GeometryBuilder] Failed geometry for shape {shape.Id} on slide {slideIndex}: {ex.Message}"
                            );
                        }
                    }
                }

                Logging.Info($"[GeometryBuilder] Geometry extraction complete. Total shapes: {shapes.Count}");
                return shapes;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Logging.Error($"[GeometryBuilder] Unexpected error: {ex.Message}");
                throw;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Builds geometric information for a single PowerPoint shape.
        /// Includes bounding box, rotation, placeholder metadata, visibility,
        /// grouping, and formatting flags.
        /// </summary>
        private static ShapeData BuildShapeGeometry(PPTShape shape, int slideIndex)
        {
            try
            {
                var shapeData = new ShapeData
                {
                    Id = shape.Id,
                    Name = shape.Name,
                    SlideIndex = slideIndex,
                    ShapeType = ConvertShapeType(shape.Type),
                    IsPlaceholder = shape.Type == MsoShapeType.msoPlaceholder,

                    PlaceholderType = shape.Type == MsoShapeType.msoPlaceholder
                        ? ConvertPlaceholderType(shape.PlaceholderFormat.Type)
                        : (PlaceholderTypeENUM?)null,

                    // Geometry
                    Left = shape.Left,
                    Top = shape.Top,
                    Width = shape.Width,
                    Height = shape.Height,
                    Rotation = shape.Rotation,

                    // Formatting flags
                    HasText = shape.HasTextFrame == MsoTriState.msoTrue,
                    HasFill = shape.Fill.Visible == MsoTriState.msoTrue,
                    HasBorder = shape.Line.Visible == MsoTriState.msoTrue,

                    // Grouping
                    IsGrouped = shape.ParentGroup != null,
                    GroupId = shape.ParentGroup != null ? shape.ParentGroup.Id : (int?)null,

                    // Visibility
                    IsHidden = shape.Visible != MsoTriState.msoTrue,

                    // Z-order
                    ZOrder = shape.ZOrderPosition
                };

                // Bounding box in EMUs
                shapeData.BoundingBox = new BoundingBoxSDTO
                {
                    X_EMU = (long)shape.Left,
                    Y_EMU = (long)shape.Top,
                    Width_EMU = (long)shape.Width,
                    Height_EMU = (long)shape.Height
                };

                return shapeData;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Logging.Warn($"[GeometryBuilder] Failed to build geometry for shape {shape.Id}: {ex.Message}");
                return null;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Converts PowerPoint's MsoShapeType into SMARTtools ShapeTypeENUM.
        /// </summary>
        private static ShapeTypeENUM ConvertShapeType(MsoShapeType type)
        {
            return type switch
            {
                MsoShapeType.msoTextBox => ShapeTypeENUM.TextBox,
                MsoShapeType.msoPicture => ShapeTypeENUM.Picture,
                MsoShapeType.msoPlaceholder => ShapeTypeENUM.Placeholder,
                MsoShapeType.msoGroup => ShapeTypeENUM.Group,
                MsoShapeType.msoMedia => ShapeTypeENUM.Media,
                _ => ShapeTypeENUM.Unknown
            };
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Converts PowerPoint placeholder types into SMARTtools PlaceholderTypeENUM.
        /// </summary>
        private static PlaceholderTypeENUM ConvertPlaceholderType(PpPlaceholderType type)
        {
            return type switch
            {
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderTitle => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Title,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderCenterTitle => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Title,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderSubtitle => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Subtitle,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderBody => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Body,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderChart => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Chart,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderTable => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Table,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderPicture => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Picture,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderMediaClip => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Media,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderFooter => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Footer,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderHeader => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Header,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderDate => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Date,
                PpPlaceholderType.ppPlaceholderSlideNumber => PlaceholderTypeENUM.SlideNumber,
                _ => PlaceholderTypeENUM.Custom
            };
        }
    }
}

//
// IssueDetector.cs
// SMARTtools.Engine.Issues
//
// Performs structural and semantic validation on the EngineContext,
// identifying missing geometry, missing metadata, broken object references,
// invalid slide structures, and other extraction inconsistencies.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools engine diagnostics layer.
//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.MetaData;
using SMARTtools.Engine;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine.Issues
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Provides diagnostic scanning across the EngineContext to detect
    /// missing geometry, missing metadata, invalid references, and other
    /// extraction inconsistencies.
    /// </summary>
    public static class IssueDetector
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Runs all issue detection routines and returns a list of Issue objects.
        /// </summary>
        public static List<Issue> DetectIssues(EngineContext context)
        {
            var issues = new List<Issue>();

            if (context == null)
            {
                issues.Add(new Issue
                {
                    Severity = IssueSeverity.Critical,
                    Message = "EngineContext is null. Cannot perform issue detection."
                });

                return issues;
            }

            DetectMissingGeometry(context, issues);
            DetectMissingMetadata(context, issues);
            DetectInvalidObjectReferences(context, issues);
            DetectSlideStructuralIssues(context, issues);

            return issues;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Detects shapes missing geometry information.
        /// </summary>
        private static void DetectMissingGeometry(EngineContext context, List<Issue> issues)
        {
            foreach (var obj in context.Objects)
            {
                if (obj.Geometry == null)
                {
                    issues.Add(new Issue
                    {
                        Severity = IssueSeverity.Warning,
                        Message = $"Object {obj.ObjectId} on slide {obj.SlideIndex} has no geometry."
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Detects slides or objects missing metadata.
        /// </summary>
        private static void DetectMissingMetadata(EngineContext context, List<Issue> issues)
        {
            foreach (var slide in context.Slides)
            {
                if (slide.Metadata == null)
                {
                    issues.Add(new Issue
                    {
                        Severity = IssueSeverity.Warning,
                        Message = $"Slide {slide.Index} has no metadata."
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Detects objects referencing invalid slide indices or missing DTOs.
        /// </summary>
        private static void DetectInvalidObjectReferences(EngineContext context, List<Issue> issues)
        {
            foreach (var obj in context.Objects)
            {
                if (obj.SlideIndex <= 0 || obj.SlideIndex > context.Slides.Count)
                {
                    issues.Add(new Issue
                    {
                        Severity = IssueSeverity.Error,
                        Message = $"Object {obj.ObjectId} references invalid slide index {obj.SlideIndex}."
                    });
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Detects structural slide issues such as missing title, missing layout,
        /// or empty object collections.
        /// </summary>
        private static void DetectSlideStructuralIssues(EngineContext context, List<Issue> issues)
        {
            foreach (var slide in context.Slides)
            {
                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(slide.LayoutName))
                {
                    issues.Add(new Issue
                    {
                        Severity = IssueSeverity.Warning,
                        Message = $"Slide {slide.Index} has no layout name."
                    });
                }

                if (slide.Objects == null || slide.Objects.Count == 0)
                {
                    issues.Add(new Issue
                    {
                        Severity = IssueSeverity.Info,
                        Message = $"Slide {slide.Index} contains no objects."
                    });
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

//
// MediaGeometryBuilder.cs
// SMARTtools.Engine.Builders
//
// Projects media geometry from the PowerPoint extraction context into
// engine-level MediaGeometryData records.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools engine builder layer.
//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction.Geometry;
using SMARTtools.Engine;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine.Builders
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Copies media geometry from the PowerPointContext into the engine's
    /// MediaGeometry collection for downstream analysis and reporting.
    /// </summary>
    public static class MediaGeometryBuilder
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Executes the media geometry projection for the current engine context.
        /// Safely returns if the PowerPointContext or its MediaGeometry collection
        /// is not available.
        /// </summary>
        public static void Run(EngineContext context)
        {
            if (context?.PowerPointContext?.MediaGeometry == null)
                return;

            foreach (var mg in context.PowerPointContext.MediaGeometry)
            {
                context.MediaGeometry.Add(new MediaGeometryData
                {
                    SlideIndex = mg.SlideIndex,
                    ShapeId = mg.ShapeId,
                    X = mg.X,
                    Y = mg.Y,
                    Width = mg.Width,
                    Height = mg.Height
                });
            }
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine.Builders
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// OGM BUILDER (v3)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Builds the Object Graph Model (OGM) from EngineContext pipeline state.
    ///
    /// ROLE:
    ///     - Understands parent/child relationships
    ///     - Understands grouping
    ///     - Understands slide structure
    ///     - Understands object hierarchy
    ///
    /// OUTPUT:
    ///     - Returns an OGM instance (EngineShellResult stores it)
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS DOES NOT DO:
    ///     ? Write into EngineContext
    ///     ? Produce DTOs
    ///     ? Detect issues
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public static class OGMBuilder
    {
        public static OGM Build(EngineContext context)
        {
            if (context == null)
                return new OGM();

            var ogm = new OGM
            {
                Slides = new List<OGMSlide>()
            };

            // Build slide-level OGM
            foreach (var slide in context.PowerPointContext.Slides)
            {
                var ogmSlide = new OGMSlide
                {
                    SlideIndex = slide.Index,
                    Objects = new List<OGMObject>()
                };

                // Build object-level OGM
                foreach (var shape in context.ShapeModels)
                {
                    if (shape.SlideIndex != slide.Index)
                        continue;

                    ogmSlide.Objects.Add(new OGMObject
                    {
                        ObjectId = shape.ObjectId,
                        ParentId = shape.GroupId,
                        ObjectType = shape.ObjectType,
                        IsInGroup = shape.IsInGroup
                    });
                }

                ogm.Slides.Add(ogmSlide);
            }

            return ogm;
        }
    }
}

//
// PlaceholderGeometryBuilder.cs
// SMARTtools.Engine.Builders
//
// Projects placeholder geometry from the PowerPoint extraction context into
// engine-level PlaceholderGeometryData records.
//
// This module is part of the stabilized SMARTtools engine builder layer.
//

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO.Geometry;
using SMARTtools.Engine;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine.Builders
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Copies placeholder geometry from the PowerPointContext into the engine's
    /// PlaceholderGeometry collection for downstream analysis and reporting.
    /// </summary>
    public static class PlaceholderGeometryBuilder
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Executes the placeholder geometry projection for the current engine context.
        /// Safely returns if the PowerPointContext or its Slides collection is not available.
        /// </summary>
        public static void Run(EngineContext context)
        {
            if (context?.PowerPointContext?.Slides == null)
                return;

            foreach (var slide in context.PowerPointContext.Slides)
            {
                foreach (var ph in slide.Placeholders)
                {
                    context.PlaceholderGeometry.Add(new PlaceholderGeometryData
                    {
                        SlideIndex = slide.Index,
                        PlaceholderType = ph.Type,
                        PlaceholderIndex = ph.Index,
                        X = ph.X,
                        Y = ph.Y,
                        Width = ph.Width,
                        Height = ph.Height
                    });
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: Engine\PlaceholderMetadataBuilder.cs

LAST MODIFIED: 2026-07-25 20:06:42

using SMARTtools.Core;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction;
using SMARTtools.Core.Pipeline;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine.Builders
{
    /// <summary>
    /// PlaceholderMetadataBuilder
    ///
    /// Bridges extraction ? pipeline by normalizing placeholder metadata
    /// and attaching it to the correct ShapeModel.
    ///
    /// INPUT:
    /// - PowerPointContext.PlaceholderMetadata (List<PlaceholderMetadataExtraction>)
    /// - ShapeModels (List<ShapeModel>)
    ///
    /// OUTPUT:
    /// - ShapeModel.PlaceholderMetadata (PlaceholderMetadataData)
    /// - ShapeModel placeholder flags populated
    /// </summary>
    public static class PlaceholderMetadataBuilder
    {
        public static void Run(EngineContext context)
        {
            if (context?.ShapeModels == null)
                return;

            if (context.PowerPointContext?.PlaceholderMetadata == null)
                return;

            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            // BUILD LOOKUP TABLE
            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            //
            // Key: (SlideIndex, ShapeId)
            // Value: PlaceholderMetadataExtraction
            //
            var lookup = new Dictionary<(int slide, int shape), PlaceholderMetadataExtraction>();

            foreach (var ph in context.PowerPointContext.PlaceholderMetadata)
            {
                lookup[(ph.SlideIndex, ph.ShapeId)] = ph;
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            // ATTACH NORMALIZED METADATA TO SHAPEMODELS
            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            foreach (var shape in context.ShapeModels)
            {
                // Only shapes marked as placeholders should receive metadata.
                if (!shape.IsPlaceholder)
                    continue;

                if (lookup.TryGetValue((shape.SlideIndex, shape.Id), out var phExtract))
                {
                    // Normalize extraction ? pipeline DTO
                    var normalized = new PlaceholderMetadataDTO
                    {
                        SlideIndex = phExtract.SlideIndex,
                        ShapeId = phExtract.ShapeId,
                        PlaceholderType = phExtract.PlaceholderType,
                        IsRequired = phExtract.IsRequired,
                        IsEmpty = phExtract.IsEmpty,
                        IsAutoGenerated = phExtract.IsAutoGenerated,
                        IsUserModified = phExtract.IsUserModified,
                        IsActive = phExtract.IsActive
                    };

                    // Attach to ShapeModel
                    shape.PlaceholderMetadata = normalized;

                    // Populate ShapeModel flags
                    shape.PlaceholderType = phExtract.PlaceholderType;
                    shape.IsRequiredPlaceholder = phExtract.IsRequired;
                    shape.IsEmptyPlaceholder = phExtract.IsEmpty;
                    shape.IsActivePlaceholder = phExtract.IsActive;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction;

namespace SMARTtools.Core.DTO
{
    /// <summary>
    /// PowerPointContext contains ALL raw extraction data from the InteropWrapper.
    /// It is the input to the SMARTtools pipeline and is consumed by EngineContext.
    ///
    /// IMPORTANT:
    ///     - This class stores ONLY extraction-level models.
    ///     - Normalized models live in EngineContext.
    ///     - DTO/SDTO mapping happens AFTER this stage.
    /// </summary>
    public class PowerPointContext
    {
        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // RAW EXTRACTION MODELS
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// All placeholder metadata extracted across the entire deck.
        /// Used by builders that operate at slide-level or deck-level.
        /// </summary>
        public List<PlaceholderMetadataExtraction> PlaceholderMetadata { get; set; }
            = new List<PlaceholderMetadataExtraction>();


        /// <summary>
        /// Slide-level extraction (index, id, layout, background, placeholders).
        /// </summary>
        public List<SlideData> Slides { get; set; }
            = new List<SlideData>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Text shapes extracted from all slides.
        /// </summary>
        public List<TextData> TextShapes { get; set; }
            = new List<TextData>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Media objects extracted from all slides.
        /// </summary>
        public List<MediaData> Media { get; set; }
            = new List<MediaData>();

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // GEOMETRY EXTRACTION (raw, not normalized)
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw geometry footprints for shapes.
        /// </summary>
        public List<GeometryData> Geometry { get; set; }
            = new List<GeometryData>();

        /// <summary>
        /// Raw geometry footprints for media objects.
        /// </summary>
        public List<MediaGeometryData> MediaGeometry { get; set; }
            = new List<MediaGeometryData>();
    }
}

using Microsoft.Office.Core;
using Microsoft.Office.Interop.PowerPoint;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;
using SMARTtools.Core.Extraction;
using SMARTtools.Utilities;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
// Alias to eliminate ambiguous references between Office.Core.Shape and PowerPoint.Shape
using PPTShape = Microsoft.Office.Interop.PowerPoint.Shape;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// PowerPointInteropWrapper handles extraction of raw data from PowerPoint presentations
    /// using Microsoft Office Interop APIs.
    /// 
    /// ROLE IN ARCHITECTURE:
    /// This is the ONLY module that directly interacts with PowerPoint Interop.
    /// All other modules consume the extraction models (SlideData, ShapeData, etc.)
    /// produced by this wrapper.
    /// 
    /// WHY THIS ISOLATION MATTERS:
    /// PowerPoint Interop is:
    /// - Notoriously quirky (crashes, COM exceptions, memory leaks)
    /// - Platform-specific (Windows-only)
    /// - Version-dependent (behavior changes between PowerPoint versions)
    /// 
    /// By isolating Interop here, we:
    /// - Make the rest of SMARTtools testable (can mock extraction models)
    /// - Contain COM weirdness to one place
    /// - Make it easier to support future PowerPoint versions
    /// 
    /// DATA FLOW:
    /// PowerPoint Presentation ? PowerPointInteropWrapper ? 
    /// Raw Extraction Models (SlideData, ShapeData, TextData, MediaData) ?
    /// PowerPointContext ? Rest of SMARTtools pipeline
    /// 
    /// USED BY:
    /// - EngineShell (calls ExtractPresentation to start pipeline)
    /// 
    /// CRITICAL FOR:
    /// - "It's the data" - If extraction is incomplete, everything fails
    /// - Device optimization - Must extract accurate geometry
    /// - Compliance - Must extract all text, media, metadata
    /// </summary>
    public static class PowerPointInteropWrapper
    {
        // =====================================================================
        // PUBLIC API
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Extracts all data from the active PowerPoint presentation.
        /// 
        /// WHY STATIC METHOD:
        /// This is a stateless extraction utility. No need for instance state.
        /// 
        /// RETURNS:
        /// PowerPointContext containing all raw extraction models.
        /// 
        /// THROWS:
        /// - InvalidOperationException: No presentation is open
        /// - COMException: PowerPoint Interop failure
        /// - Exception: Unexpected errors during extraction
        /// 
        /// PERFORMANCE:
        /// Large presentations (100+ slides) may take 5-10 seconds.
        /// This is unavoidable - PowerPoint Interop is slow.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="presentation">The PowerPoint presentation to extract data from.</param>
        /// <returns>PowerPointContext containing all extracted data.</returns>
        public static PowerPointContext ExtractPresentation(Presentation presentation)
        {
            // Validate input
            if (presentation == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(presentation),
                    "Presentation cannot be null. Ensure a presentation is open in PowerPoint.");
            }

            Logging.Info("[PowerPointInteropWrapper] Starting presentation extraction...");

            try
            {
                // Create the context that will hold all extracted data
                var context = new PowerPointContext();

                // EXTRACTION PIPELINE:
                // We extract in this order because later stages may reference earlier data
                // (e.g., text extraction needs to know which shapes exist)

                // 1. Extract slides (metadata, layout, background)
                context.Slides = ExtractSlides(presentation);
                Logging.Info($"[PowerPointInteropWrapper] Extracted {context.Slides.Count} slides");

                // 2. Extract text shapes (content, fonts, formatting)
                context.TextShapes = ExtractTextShapes(presentation);
                Logging.Info($"[PowerPointInteropWrapper] Extracted {context.TextShapes.Count} text shapes");

                // 3. Extract media (images, videos, audio)
                context.Media = ExtractMedia(presentation);
                Logging.Info($"[PowerPointInteropWrapper] Extracted {context.Media.Count} media objects");

                // 4. Extract geometry (positions, sizes, relationships)
                // NOTE: Geometry extraction happens in GeometryBuilder, not here.
                // We extract raw shape data, GeometryBuilder processes it into GeometryData.
                // This separation keeps extraction simple and geometry calculations isolated.

                Logging.Info("[PowerPointInteropWrapper] Extraction complete");

                return context;
            }
            catch (System.Runtime.InteropServices.COMException comEx)
            {
                // COM exceptions are common with PowerPoint Interop
                Logging.Error($"[PowerPointInteropWrapper] COM error during extraction: {comEx.Message}");
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    "Failed to extract presentation data due to PowerPoint Interop error. " +
                    "This may indicate a corrupted presentation or PowerPoint version incompatibility.",
                    comEx);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Unexpected errors
                Logging.Error($"[PowerPointInteropWrapper] Unexpected error during extraction: {ex.Message}");
                throw;
            }
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // SLIDE EXTRACTION
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Extracts slide-level data from all slides in the presentation.
        /// 
        /// WHY WE EXTRACT SLIDES FIRST:
        /// Slides are the containers for all other objects. We need slide metadata
        /// (index, layout, background) before we can extract shapes/text/media.
        /// 
        /// POWERPOINT QUIRK:
        /// Slide indexing is 1-based (Slide 1, Slide 2, etc.), not 0-based.
        /// We preserve this convention to match PowerPoint's behavior.
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="presentation">The PowerPoint presentation.</param>
        /// <returns>List of SlideData objects, one per slide.</returns>
        private static List<SlideData> ExtractSlides(Presentation presentation)
        {
            var slides = new List<SlideData>();

            try
            {
                // Loop through all slides (1-based indexing)
                for (int i = 1; i <= presentation.Slides.Count; i++)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        var slide = presentation.Slides[i];
                        var slideData = new SlideData
                        {
                            // IDENTITY
                            Index = i, // 1-based to match PowerPoint
                            SlideId = slide.SlideID,

                            // LAYOUT & MASTER
                            LayoutName = GetLayoutName(slide),
                            LayoutType = ClassifyLayout(slide),
                            MasterName = GetMasterName(slide),

                            // CONTENT FLAGS
                            HasTitlePlaceholder = HasTitlePl

using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;              // OGM, Issue, and all geometry/text DTOs live here.
using System.Collections.Generic;       // Needed for List<T>

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// SERVER PAYLOAD DTO
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// This class represents the final export structure sent to SMARTshare / SMARTcast.
    ///
    /// WHAT IT CONTAINS:
    /// - OGM (Object Geometry Model) for the slide deck
    /// - Issues detected by IssueDetector
    /// - Versioning metadata
    /// - Export timestamp
    /// - Source application identifier
    ///
    /// WHAT IT DOES NOT CONTAIN:
    /// - Raw PowerPoint data
    /// - COM objects
    /// - Extraction artifacts
    ///
    /// This DTO is intentionally simple and stable. It is the "wire format" for SMARTshare.
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public class ServerPayload
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Version of the payload format.
        /// Explicit versioning allows future evolution without breaking older clients.
        /// </summary>
        public string Version { get; set; } = "1.0.0";

        /// <summary>
        /// The fully interpreted Object Geometry Model for the deck.
        /// Produced by OGMBuilder.
        /// </summary>
        public OGM OGM { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// All issues detected by IssueDetector.
        /// </summary>
        public List<Issue> Issues { get; set; }

        /// <summary>
        /// Identifies the originating engine.
        /// Useful for multi?engine SMARTshare ingestion.
        /// </summary>
        public string SourceApplication { get; set; } = "SMARTtools";

        /// <summary>
        /// ISO?8601 UTC timestamp marking when the payload was generated.
        /// </summary>
        public string ExportTimestamp { get; set; }
    }
}

using System;
using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// SERVER PAYLOAD BUILDER (v3)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// Converts an EngineShellResult into a ServerPayload suitable for ingestion
    /// by the Big Four:
    ///     - SMARTshare
    ///     - SMARTcomply
    ///     - SMARTcast
    ///     - SMARTmanage
    ///
    /// ROLE IN PIPELINE:
    ///     Extraction ? Normalize ? Map ? Build ? Assemble ? Payload ? Publish
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS DOES:
    ///     ? Copies OGM
    ///     ? Copies Issues
    ///     ? Copies DeckDTO (full DTO footprint)
    ///     ? Stamps version + timestamp
    ///     ? Adds source metadata
    ///
    /// WHAT THIS DOES NOT DO:
    ///     ? Modify OGM
    ///     ? Modify Issues
    ///     ? Modify DTOs
    ///     ? Perform validation
    ///     ? Perform compliance logic
    ///
    /// This module is intentionally simple and future?proof.
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public static class ServerPayloadBuilder
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Builds a fully?formed ServerPayload from EngineShellResult.
        ///
        /// SAFETY:
        ///     - Returns null if result is null.
        ///     - Timestamp is always ISO?8601 UTC.
        /// </summary>
        public static ServerPayload Build(EngineShellResult result)
        {
            if (result == null)
                return null;

            var payload = new ServerPayload
            {
                // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                // VERSIONING + METADATA
                // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                Version = "1.0.0",                                  // Explicit versioning for future evolution.
                SourceApplication = result.SourceApplication,        // Identifies originating engine.
                ExportTimestamp = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o"),     // ISO?8601 UTC timestamp.

                // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                // CORE OUTPUT MODELS
                // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
                OGM = result.OGM,                                    // Full object graph model.
                Issues = result.Issues,                              // All detected issues.
                Deck = result.Deck                                   // Full DTO footprint of the deck.
            };

            return payload;
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using SMARTtools.Core;
using SMARTtools.Core.Enums;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// SlidePlaceholderNormalizer
    ///
    /// Aggregates placeholder semantics at the slide level.
    /// Consumes ShapeModel placeholder metadata and produces:
    /// - Placeholder grouping
    /// - Required/optional lists
    /// - Empty/active lists
    /// - Missing/extra placeholder flags
    /// - Semantic grouping by PlaceholderTypeENUM
    ///
    /// This module prepares SlideModel for:
    /// - IssueDetector
    /// - SMARTaudit
    /// - SMARTshare
    /// - SMARTcomply
    /// - OGMBuilder
    /// </summary>
    public static class SlidePlaceholderNormalizer
    {
        public static void Run(EngineContext context)
        {
            // Use the correct property name from EngineContext
            if (context?.SlideModels == null)
                return;

            foreach (var slide in context.SlideModels)
            {
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Collect placeholder shapes
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                var placeholders = new List<ShapeModel>();
                var required = new List<ShapeModel>();
                var optional = new List<ShapeModel>();
                var empty = new List<ShapeModel>();
                var active = new List<ShapeModel>();
                var byType = new Dictionary<PlaceholderTypeENUM, List<ShapeModel>>();

                foreach (var shape in slide.Shapes)
                {
                    if (!shape.IsPlaceholder)
                        continue;

                    placeholders.Add(shape);

                    // Required vs optional
                    if (shape.IsRequiredPlaceholder)
                        required.Add(shape);
                    else
                        optional.Add(shape);

                    // Empty vs active
                    if (shape.IsEmptyPlaceholder)
                        empty.Add(shape);
                    else if (shape.IsActivePlaceholder)
                        active.Add(shape);

                    // Group by placeholder type
                    if (!byType.ContainsKey(shape.PlaceholderType))
                        byType[shape.PlaceholderType] = new List<ShapeModel>();

                    byType[shape.PlaceholderType].Add(shape);
                }

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Assign to SlideModel
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                slide.PlaceholderShapes = placeholders;
                slide.RequiredPlaceholders = required;
                slide.OptionalPlaceholders = optional;
                slide.EmptyPlaceholders = empty;
                slide.ActivePlaceholders = active;
                slide.PlaceholdersByType = byType;

                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                // Compliance flags
                // ------------------------------------------------------------
                slide.HasMissingRequiredPlaceholders = required.Count == 0;
                slide.HasExtraPlaceholders = optional.Count > 0 && placeholders.Count > required.Count;
            }
        }
    }
}
