using SMARTtools.Core.DTO;
using SMARTtools.Core;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// TEXT NORMALIZATION MODULE (FULL REGEN)
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// This module transforms raw PowerPoint text into a structured, cleaned, bullet-aware representation.
    ///
    /// INPUT:
    ///     - TextData (raw text extracted from PowerPoint)
    ///
    /// OUTPUT:
    ///     - NormalizedText (cleaned text, paragraphs, bullet metadata)
    ///     - TextNormalizationMetrics (statistics about normalization)
    ///
    /// ARCHITECTURE NOTES:
    ///     ? Pure text module (no geometry)
    ///     ? Bullet-aware
    ///     ? Paragraph-aware
    ///     ? Metrics-rich
    ///     ? Stable contract for IssueDetector + OGMBuilder
    ///
    /// PIPELINE ROLE:
    ///     Extraction ? TextData.RawText
    ///     Normalization ? NormalizedText
    ///     Issues ? IssueDetector
    ///     Output ? ServerPayloadBuilder
    ///
    /// ====================================================================================================
    /// </summary>
    public static class TextNormalization
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Normalizes a single text shape.
        /// </summary>
        public static NormalizedText Normalize(TextData raw)
        {
            if (raw == null)
                return null;

            var normalized = new NormalizedText
            {
                SlideIndex = raw.SlideIndex,
                ShapeId = raw.ShapeId,
                Metrics = new TextNormalizationMetrics()
            };

            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            // STEP 1: Clean raw text
            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            string cleaned = CleanText(raw.RawText);
            normalized.CleanText = cleaned;

            // Metrics
            normalized.Metrics.RawLength = raw.RawText?.Length ?? 0;
            normalized.Metrics.CleanLength = cleaned.Length;

            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            // STEP 2: Split into paragraphs
            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            var paragraphs = SplitIntoParagraphs(cleaned);

            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            // STEP 3: Analyze bullet structure
            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            foreach (var p in paragraphs)
            {
                var para = AnalyzeParagraph(p);
                normalized.Paragraphs.Add(para);

                if (para.IsBullet)
                    normalized.Metrics.BulletCount++;
            }

            normalized.Metrics.ParagraphCount = normalized.Paragraphs.Count;

            return normalized;
        }

        // ====================================================================================================
        // TEXT CLEANING
        // ====================================================================================================

        private static string CleanText(string raw)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(raw))
                return string.Empty;

            string cleaned = raw;

            // Trim whitespace
            cleaned = cleaned.Trim();

            // Collapse multiple spaces
            cleaned = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Replace(cleaned, @"\s{2,}", " ");

            // Normalize line breaks
            cleaned = cleaned.Replace("\r\n", "\n").Replace("\r", "\n");

            return cleaned;
        }

        // ====================================================================================================
        // PARAGRAPH SPLITTING
        // ====================================================================================================

        private static List<string> SplitIntoParagraphs(string cleaned)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cleaned))
                return new List<string>();

            return cleaned
                .Split('\n')
                .Select(p => p.Trim())
                .Where(p => p.Length > 0)
                .ToList();
        }

        // ====================================================================================================
        // PARAGRAPH ANALYSIS (FULL RESTORE)
        // ====================================================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Analyzes a single paragraph of cleaned text and determines:
        ///     - bullet presence
        ///     - bullet type (dash, dot, number)
        ///     - bullet level (indentation)
        ///     - cleaned paragraph text
        ///
        /// This method restores the legacy pipeline behavior while remaining
        /// compatible with the new NormalizedParagraph model.
        /// </summary>
        private static NormalizedParagraph AnalyzeParagraph(string text)
        {
            var para = new NormalizedParagraph
            {
                Text = text,
                IsBullet = false,
                BulletType = null,
                BulletLevel = 0,
                IsAutoBullet = false
            };

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
                return para;

            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            // BULLET DETECTION
            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            if (IsBulletDash(text))
            {
                para.IsBullet = true;
                para.BulletType = "dash";
            }
            else if (IsBulletDot(text))
            {
                para.IsBullet = true;
                para.BulletType = "dot";
            }
            else if (IsBulletNumber(text))
            {
                para.IsBullet = true;
                para.BulletType = "number";
            }

            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            // BULLET LEVEL (indentation)
            // ------------------------------------------------------------
            para.BulletLevel = DetectBulletLevel(text);

            return para;
        }

        // ====================================================================================================
        // BULLET HELPERS (FULL RESTORE)
        // ====================================================================================================

        private static bool IsBulletDash(string text)
        {
            return text.StartsWith("- ") || text.StartsWith("– ") || text.StartsWith("— ");
        }

        private static bool IsBulletDot(string text)
        {
            return text.StartsWith("• ") || text.StartsWith("* ");
        }

        private static bool IsBulletNumber(string text)
        {
            return char.IsDigit(text.FirstOrDefault()) &&
                   (text.Contains(". ") || text.Contains(") ") || text.Contains(" - "));
        }

        private static int DetectBulletLevel(string text)
        {
            int spaces = text.TakeWhile(c => c == ' ').Count();

            if (spaces >= 12) return 3;
            if (spaces >= 8) return 2;
            if (spaces >= 4) return 1;
            return 0;
        }
    }
}

using SMARTtools.Engine;

namespace SMARTtools.Engine.Builders
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Normalizes raw text into a consistent internal representation.
    /// </summary>
    public static class TextNormalizationBuilder
    {
        public static void Run(EngineContext context)
        {
            if (context?.PowerPointContext?.TextShapes == null)
                return;

            foreach (var t in context.PowerPointContext.TextShapes)
            {
                context.NormalizedText.Add(new NormalizedText
                {
                    SlideIndex = t.SlideIndex,
                    ShapeId = t.ShapeId,
                    RawText = t.RawText?.Trim()
                });
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using SMARTtools.Control;

namespace SMARTtools.Installation
{
    internal static class Bootstrapper
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Ensures SMARTtools is installed and meets the required version.
        /// Returns true if SMARTtools is ready for use.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool EnsureSMARTtools(string requiredVersion)
        {
            // Check if SMARTtools is installed
            if (!VersionCheck.IsInstalled())
            {
                // SMARTtools is not installed
                return false;
            }

            // Check version compatibility
            if (!VersionCheck.IsCompatible(requiredVersion))
            {
                // SMARTtools is installed but outdated
                return false;
            }

            return true;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Returns the installed SMARTtools version, or null if not installed.
        /// </summary>
        public static Version GetInstalledVersion()
        {
            return VersionCheck.GetInstalledVersion();
        }
    }
}

using System;

namespace SMARTtools.Installation
{
    internal static class Initializer
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Initializes SMARTtools and ensures the platform is ready for use.
        /// Returns true if SMARTtools is installed, healthy, and compatible.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool Initialize()
        {
            // Check installation state
            if (!State.IsInstalled())
            {
                Log("SMARTtools is not installed.");
                return false;
            }

            // Check version validity
            if (!State.HasValidVersion())
            {
                Log("SMARTtools installation is incomplete or corrupted.");
                return false;
            }

            // Check compatibility
            if (!VersionCheck.IsCompatible(Manifest.MinimumRequiredVersion))
            {
                Log("SMARTtools is installed but outdated.");
                return false;
            }

            // SMARTtools is ready
            Log($"SMARTtools initialized successfully (version {Manifest.CurrentVersion}).");
            return true;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Simple logging hook for initialization diagnostics.
        /// Replace with SMARTtools.Utilities.Logging later.
        /// </summary>
        private static void Log(string message)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[SMARTtools Init] {message}");
        }
    }
}

using Microsoft.Win32;
using SMARTtools.Control;

namespace SMARTtools.Installation
{
    internal static class Installer
    {
        private const string RegistryPath = @"Software\SMARTtools\Platform";

        public static void WriteVersion()
        {
            using (var key = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey(RegistryPath))
            {
                key.SetValue("Version", PlatformVersion.Version, RegistryValueKind.String);
            }
        }

        public static string ReadVersion()
        {
            using (var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(RegistryPath))
            {
                return key?.GetValue("Version") as string;
            }
        }
    }

using System;
using Microsoft.Win32;

namespace SMARTtools.Installation
{
    internal static class State
    {
        private const string RegistryPath = Manifest.RegistryPath;

        /// <summary>
        /// Returns true if SMARTtools is installed (registry key exists).
        /// </summary>
        public static bool IsInstalled()
        {
            using (var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(RegistryPath))
            {
                return key != null;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Returns true if SMARTtools has a valid version entry.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool HasValidVersion()
        {
            using (var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(RegistryPath))
            {
                if (key == null)
                    return false;

                var versionString = key.GetValue("Version") as string;
                return !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(versionString);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Returns true if SMARTtools is installed and version is compatible.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool IsHealthy()
        {
            if (!IsInstalled())
                return false;

            if (!HasValidVersion())
                return false;

            return VersionCheck.IsCompatible(Manifest.MinimumRequiredVersion);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Returns a human-readable state string for diagnostics.
        /// </summary>
        public static string Describe()
        {
            if (!IsInstalled())
                return "SMARTtools is not installed.";

            if (!HasValidVersion())
                return "SMARTtools installation is incomplete or corrupted.";

            var installedVersion = VersionCheck.GetInstalledVersion();
            var requiredVersion = new Version(Manifest.MinimumRequiredVersion);

            if (installedVersion < requiredVersion)
                return $"SMARTtools is outdated (installed: {installedVersion}, required: {requiredVersion}).";

            return $"SMARTtools is installed and healthy (version {installedVersion}).";
        }
    }
}

using System;
using Microsoft.Win32;
using SMARTtools.Control;

namespace SMARTtools.Installation
{
    internal static class VersionCheck
    {
        private const string RegistryPath = @"Software\SMARTtools\Platform";

        public static bool IsInstalled()
        {
            using (var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(RegistryPath))
            {
                return key != null;
            }
        }

        public static bool IsCompatible(string requiredVersion)
        {
            using (var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(RegistryPath))
            {
                if (key == null)
                    return false; // SMARTtools not installed

                var installedVersionString = key.GetValue("Version") as string;
                if (installedVersionString == null)
                    return false;

                var installed = new Version(installedVersionString);
                var required = new Version(requiredVersion);

                return installed >= required;
            }
        }

        public static Version GetInstalledVersion()
        {
            using (var key = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(RegistryPath))
            {
                var installedVersionString = key?.GetValue("Version") as string;
                return installedVersionString != null ? new Version(installedVersionString) : null;
            }
        }
    }
}

    The primary goals of this format is to allow a simple XML format 
    that is mostly human readable. The generation and parsing of the 
    various data types are done through the TypeConverter classes 
    associated with the data types.
    
    Example:
    
    ... ado.net/XML headers & schema ...

    <resheader name="version">2.0</resheader>

    name/value pairs.

            : using a System.ComponentModel.TypeConverter
            : and then encoded with base64 encoding.

using System;
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace SMARTtools.Utilities
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Centralized logging utility for SMARTtools.
    /// Provides simple, consistent logging methods used across the Engine.
    /// </summary>
    public static class Logging
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Writes an informational message to the Output window.
        /// </summary>
        public static void Info(string message)
        {
            Debug.WriteLine($"[INFO] {message}");
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Writes a warning message to the Output window.
        /// </summary>
        public static void Warn(string message)
        {
            Debug.WriteLine($"[WARN] {message}");
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Writes an error message to the Output window.
        /// </summary>
        public static void Error(string message)
        {
            Debug.WriteLine($"[ERROR] {message}");
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Writes a raw diagnostic message (legacy support).
        /// </summary>
        public static void Write(string message)
        {
            Debug.WriteLine(message);
        }
    }
}
